### [167] [From Absolute to Relative: Rethinking Reward Shaping in Group-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23058)
*Wenzhe Niu,Wei He,Zongxia Xie,Jinpeng Ou,Huichuan Fan,Yuchen Ge,Yanru Sun,Ziyin Wang,Yizhao Sun,Chengshun Shi,Jiuchong Gao,Jinghua Hao,Renqing He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Reinforcement learning has become a cornerstone for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models, where group-based approaches such as GRPO have emerged as efficient paradigms that optimize policies by leveraging intra-group performance differences. However, these methods typically rely on absolute numerical rewards, introducing intrinsic limitations. In verifiable tasks, identical group evaluations often result in sparse supervision, while in open-ended scenarios, the score range instability of reward models undermines advantage estimation based on group means. To address these limitations, we propose Reinforcement Learning with Relative Rewards (RLRR), a framework that shifts reward shaping from absolute scoring to relative ranking. Complementing this framework, we introduce the Ranking Reward Model, a listwise preference model tailored for group-based optimization to directly generate relative rankings. By transforming raw evaluations into robust relative signals, RLRR effectively mitigates signal sparsity and reward instability. Experimental results demonstrate that RLRR yields consistent performance improvements over standard group-based baselines across reasoning benchmarks and open-ended generation tasks.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

However, these methods typically rely on absolute numerical rewards, introducing intrinsic limitations. In verifiable tasks, identical group evaluations often result in sparse supervision, while in open-ended scenarios, the score range instability of reward models undermines advantage estimation based on group means. To address these limitations, we propose Reinforcement Learning with Relative Rewards (RLRR), a framework that shifts reward shaping from absolute scoring to relative ranking. Complementing this framework, we introduce the Ranking Reward Model, a listwise preference model tailored for group-based optimization to directly generate relative rankings. By transforming raw evaluations into robust relative signals, RLRR effectively mitigates signal sparsity and reward instability. ### [168] [SplineFlow: Flow Matching for Dynamical Systems with B-Spline Interpolants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23072)
*Santanu Subhash Rathod,Pietro Liò,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flow matching is a scalable generative framework for characterizing continuous normalizing flows with wide-range applications. However, current state-of-the-art methods are not well-suited for modeling dynamical systems, as they construct conditional paths using linear interpolants that may not capture the underlying state evolution, especially when learning higher-order dynamics from irregular sampled observations. Constructing unified paths that satisfy multi-marginal constraints across observations is challenging, since naïve higher-order polynomials tend to be unstable and oscillatory. We introduce SplineFlow, a theoretically grounded flow matching algorithm that jointly models conditional paths across observations via B-spline interpolation. Specifically, SplineFlow exploits the smoothness and stability of B-spline bases to learn the complex underlying dynamics in a structured manner while ensuring the multi-marginal requirements are met. Comprehensive experiments across various deterministic and stochastic dynamical systems of varying complexity, as well as on cellular trajectory inference tasks, demonstrate the strong improvement of SplineFlow over existing baselines. Our code is available at: https://github.com/santanurathod/SplineFlow.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Constructing unified paths that satisfy multi-marginal constraints across observations is challenging, since naïve higher-order polynomials tend to be unstable and oscillatory. We introduce SplineFlow, a theoretically grounded flow matching algorithm that jointly models conditional paths across observations via B-spline interpolation. Specifically, SplineFlow exploits the smoothness and stability of B-spline bases to learn the complex underlying dynamics in a structured manner while ensuring the multi-marginal requirements are met. Comprehensive experiments across various deterministic and stochastic dynamical systems of varying complexity, as well as on cellular trajectory inference tasks, demonstrate the strong improvement of SplineFlow over existing baselines. Our code is available at: https://github.com/santanurathod/SplineFlow.

### [169] [RN-D: Discretized Categorical Actors with Regularized Networks for On-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23075)
*Yuexin Bian,Jie Feng,Tao Wang,Yijiang Li,Sicun Gao,Yuanyuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: On-policy deep reinforcement learning remains a dominant paradigm for continuous control, yet standard implementations rely on Gaussian actors and relatively shallow MLP policies, often leading to brittle optimization when gradients are noisy and policy updates must be conservative. In this paper, we revisit policy representation as a first-class design choice for on-policy optimization. We study discretized categorical actors that represent each action dimension with a distribution over bins, yielding a policy objective that resembles a cross-entropy loss. Building on architectural advances from supervised learning, we further propose regularized actor networks, while keeping critic design fixed. Our results show that simply replacing the standard actor network with our discretized regularized actor yields consistent gains and achieve the state-of-the-art performance across diverse continuous-control benchmarks.

Abstract: On-policy deep reinforcement learning remains a dominant paradigm for continuous control, yet standard implementations rely on Gaussian actors and relatively shallow MLP policies, often leading to brittle optimization when gradients are noisy and policy updates must be conservative. In this paper, we revisit policy representation as a first-class design choice for on-policy optimization. We study discretized categorical actors that represent each action dimension with a distribution over bins, yielding a policy objective that resembles a cross-entropy loss. Building on architectural advances from supervised learning, we further propose regularized actor networks, while keeping critic design fixed. Our results show that simply replacing the standard actor network with our discretized regularized actor yields consistent gains and achieve the state-of-the-art performance across diverse continuous-control benchmarks.

### [170] [CATTO: Balancing Preferences and Confidence in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23096)
*Nisarg Parikh,Kunjal Panchal,Ananya Sai,Pannaga Shivaswamy,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CATTO（校准感知的词级训练目标）和Confidence@k（基于校准概率的测试时扩展机制），以解决大语言模型校准不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型往往能做出准确的下一个词预测，但其预测置信度校准不佳：高置信度预测经常出错，而低置信度预测可能是正确的。基于偏好的对齐方法进一步破坏了预测概率与正确性之间的联系，加剧了这种误校准问题。

Method: 提出了CATTO——一个将预测置信度与经验预测正确性对齐的校准感知目标，可与原始偏好优化目标结合使用。同时引入Confidence@k作为测试时扩展机制，利用校准后的词级概率进行贝叶斯最优的输出词选择。

Result: 相比直接偏好优化，CATTO将预期校准误差降低了2.22%-7.61%（域内）和1.46%-10.44%（域外）；相比最强DPO基线，降低0.22%-1.24%（域内）和1.23%-5.07%（域外）。校准改进并未牺牲任务准确性，在五个数据集上保持或略微提升了多项选择问答准确性。

Conclusion: CATTO是一种有效的校准感知训练目标，能显著提升大语言模型置信度校准质量，且保持任务性能。Confidence@k机制为基于校准概率的测试时决策提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often make accurate next token predictions but their confidence in these predictions can be poorly calibrated: high-confidence predictions are frequently wrong, and low-confidence predictions may be correct. This miscalibration is exacerbated by preference-based alignment methods breaking the link between predictive probability and correctness. We introduce a Calibration Aware Token-level Training Objective (CATTO), a calibration-aware objective that aligns predicted confidence with empirical prediction correctness, which can be combined with the original preference optimization objectives. Empirically, CATTO reduces Expected Calibration Error (ECE) by 2.22%-7.61% in-distribution and 1.46%-10.44% out-of-distribution compared to direct preference optimization (DPO), and by 0.22%-1.24% in-distribution and 1.23%-5.07% out-of-distribution compared to the strongest DPO baseline. This improvement in confidence does not come at a cost of losing task accuracy, where CATTO maintains or slightly improves multiple-choice question-answering accuracy on five datasets. We also introduce Confidence@k, a test-time scaling mechanism leveraging calibrated token probabilities for Bayes-optimal selection of output tokens.

</details>


### [171] [Distribution-informed Efficient Conformal Prediction for Full Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23128)
*Wenbo Liao,Huipeng Huang,Chen Jia,Huajun Xi,Hao Zeng,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出了分布感知的保形排序，解决了现有保形排序中的保守性问题，显著缩小了预测集大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的保形排序方法依赖非一致性得分上界，导致预测集过大而过于保守，在确保覆盖度同时需要提升效率。

Method: 提出分布感知的保形排序，利用负超几何分布推导非一致性得分的精确分布，从而确定更准确的分位数临界值构建预测集。

Result: DCR在保证覆盖度的同时，将平均预测集大小降低达36%，在多项实验中验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该文提供了一种更高效的保形排序框架，通过精确模拟非一致性得分分布显著提升预测集效率，理论保证和实验结果均验证其优势。

Abstract: Quantifying uncertainty is critical for the safe deployment of ranking models in real-world applications. Recent work offers a rigorous solution using conformal prediction in a full ranking scenario, which aims to construct prediction sets for the absolute ranks of test items based on the relative ranks of calibration items. However, relying on upper bounds of non-conformity scores renders the method overly conservative, resulting in substantially large prediction sets. To address this, we propose Distribution-informed Conformal Ranking (DCR), which produces efficient prediction sets by deriving the exact distribution of non-conformity scores. In particular, we find that the absolute ranks of calibration items follow Negative Hypergeometric distributions, conditional on their relative ranks. DCR thus uses the rank distribution to derive non-conformity score distribution and determine conformal thresholds. We provide theoretical guarantees that DCR achieves improved efficiency over the baseline while ensuring valid coverage under mild assumptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of DCR, reducing average prediction set size by up to 36%, while maintaining valid coverage.

</details>


### [172] [Why GRPO Needs Normalization: A Local-Curvature Perspective on Adaptive Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23135)
*Cheng Ge,Caitlyn Heqi Yin,Hao Liang,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key driver of language model reasoning. Among RL algorithms, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is the de facto standard, avoiding the need for a critic by using per-prompt baselines and variance normalization. Yet why and when this normalization helps remains unclear. In this work, we provide an explanation through the lens of local curvature of the sequence-level policy gradient: standard deviation normalization implements an adaptive gradient. Theoretically, under mild conditions, GRPO enjoys a strictly improved convergence rate over unnormalized REINFORCE, with gains characterized by the average within-prompt reward standard deviation across prompts and iterations. Empirically, our analysis on GSM8K and MATH benchmarks reveals three distinct training phases governed by the interplay between feature orthogonality and reward variance: (I) an early acceleration phase where high variance and orthogonality favor adaptive scaling; (II) a relatively stable transition phase; and (III) a late-stage regime where the loss of orthogonality limits further gains. Together, these results provide a principled account of when std normalization helps in GRPO, and offer broader insights into the design of critic-free RL algorithms.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key driver of language model reasoning. Among RL algorithms, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is the de facto standard, avoiding the need for a critic by using per-prompt baselines and variance normalization. Yet why and when this normalization helps remains unclear. In this work, we provide an explanation through the lens of local curvature of the sequence-level policy gradient: standard deviation normalization implements an adaptive gradient. Theoretically, under mild conditions, GRPO enjoys a strictly improved convergence rate over unnormalized REINFORCE, with gains characterized by the average within-prompt reward standard deviation across prompts and iterations. Empirically, our analysis on GSM8K and MATH benchmarks reveals three distinct training phases governed by the interplay between feature orthogonality and reward variance: (I) an early acceleration phase where high variance and orthogonality favor adaptive scaling; (II) a relatively stable transition phase; and (III) a late-stage regime where the loss of orthogonality limits further gains. Together, these results provide a principled account of when std normalization helps in GRPO, and offer broader insights into the design of critic-free RL algorithms.

</details>


### [173] [Manifold-Aware Perturbations for Constrained Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23151)
*Katherine Keegan,Lars Ruthotto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Generative models have enjoyed widespread success in a variety of applications. However, they encounter inherent mathematical limitations in modeling distributions where samples are constrained by equalities, as is frequently the setting in scientific domains. In this work, we develop a computationally cheap, mathematically justified, and highly flexible distributional modification for combating known pitfalls in equality-constrained generative models. We propose perturbing the data distribution in a constraint-aware way such that the new distribution has support matching the ambient space dimension while still implicitly incorporating underlying manifold geometry. Through theoretical analyses and empirical evidence on several representative tasks, we illustrate that our approach consistently enables data distribution recovery and stable sampling with both diffusion models and normalizing flows.

### [174] [Behemoth: Benchmarking Unlearning in LLMs Using Fully Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23153)
*Eugenia Iofinova,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Behemoth的完全合成数据生成框架，用于系统研究大语言模型的编辑机制及其与训练数据分布的交互关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在现实应用中的部署越来越多，但常产生不准确或不良输出。虽然已有模型编辑技术可修改特定事实的预测，但这些编辑方法存在脆弱性和不完整性。由于大型语言模型的复杂性以及其训练数据分布难以理解，我们无法充分理解模型编辑的效果及其与训练数据分布的交互关系。

Method: 提出Behemoth框架——一个完全合成数据生成框架，可在受控环境下研究模型编辑机制。通过在简单的表格数据上探索模型编辑，验证框架的实用性。

Result: 实验发现了一些与真实世界结果相似的意外发现，例如在某些情况下，限制更新秩rank能获得更有效的更新效果。

Conclusion: Behemoth框架为深入理解模型编辑机制及其与训练数据分布的相互作用提供了系统研究工具，有助于设计更可靠的模型编辑方法。本文代码已开源。

Abstract: As artificial neural networks, and specifically large language models, have improved rapidly in capabilities and quality, they have increasingly been deployed in real-world applications, from customer service to Google search, despite the fact that they frequently make factually incorrect or undesirable statements. This trend has inspired practical and academic interest in model editing, that is, in adjusting the weights of the model to modify its likely outputs for queries relating to a specific fact or set of facts. This may be done either to amend a fact or set of facts, for instance, to fix a frequent error in the training data, or to suppress a fact or set of facts entirely, for instance, in case of dangerous knowledge. Multiple methods have been proposed to do such edits. However, at the same time, it has been shown that such model editing can be brittle and incomplete. Moreover the effectiveness of any model editing method necessarily depends on the data on which the model is trained, and, therefore, a good understanding of the interaction of the training data distribution and the way it is stored in the network is necessary and helpful to reliably perform model editing. However, working with large language models trained on real-world data does not allow us to understand this relationship or fully measure the effects of model editing. We therefore propose Behemoth, a fully synthetic data generation framework. To demonstrate the practical insights from the framework, we explore model editing in the context of simple tabular data, demonstrating surprising findings that, in some cases, echo real-world results, for instance, that in some cases restricting the update rank results in a more effective update. The code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/behemoth.git.

</details>


### [175] [Unsupervised Hierarchical Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23156)
*Damion Harvey,Geraud Nangue Tasse,Branden Ingram,Benjamin Rosman,Steven James*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无监督技能分割和层次结构发现方法，用于强化学习中的轨迹分割和层次技能学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法大多依赖动作标签、奖励或手工标注，限制了其适用性；需要一种能够在无标签轨迹中自动发现技能和层次结构的方法。

Method: 使用基于语法的方法对无标签轨迹进行分割，并诱导生成层次技能结构，可捕捉低层行为和其组合成高层技能。

Result: 在高维像素环境（Craftax和完整原版Minecraft）中评估，在技能分割、重用和层次质量指标上优于现有基线；发现的层次结构能加速下游强化学习任务的收敛和稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方法能自动从无标签轨迹中发现结构化和语义化的技能层次，提高强化学习效率。

Abstract: We consider the problem of unsupervised skill segmentation and hierarchical structure discovery in reinforcement learning. While recent approaches have sought to segment trajectories into reusable skills or options, most rely on action labels, rewards, or handcrafted annotations, limiting their applicability. We propose a method that segments unlabelled trajectories into skills and induces a hierarchical structure over them using a grammar-based approach. The resulting hierarchy captures both low-level behaviours and their composition into higher-level skills. We evaluate our approach in high-dimensional, pixel-based environments, including Craftax and the full, unmodified version of Minecraft. Using metrics for skill segmentation, reuse, and hierarchy quality, we find that our method consistently produces more structured and semantically meaningful hierarchies than existing baselines. Furthermore, as a proof of concept for utility, we demonstrate that these discovered hierarchies accelerate and stabilise learning on downstream reinforcement learning tasks.

</details>


### [176] [Stochastic Linear Bandits with Parameter Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23164)
*Daniel Ezer,Alon Peled-Cohen,Yishay Mansour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We study the stochastic linear bandits with parameter noise model, in which the reward of action $a$ is $a^\top θ$ where $θ$ is sampled i.i.d. We show a regret upper bound of $\widetilde{O} (\sqrt{d T \log (K/δ) σ^2_{\max})}$ for a horizon $T$, general action set of size $K$ of dimension $d$, and where $σ^2_{\max}$ is the maximal variance of the reward for any action. We further provide a lower bound of $\widetildeΩ (d \sqrt{T σ^2_{\max}})$ which is tight (up to logarithmic factors) whenever $\log (K) \approx d$. For more specific action sets, $\ell_p$ unit balls with $p \leq 2$ and dual norm $q$, we show that the minimax regret is $\widetildeΘ (\sqrt{dT σ^2_q)}$, where $σ^2_q$ is a variance-dependent quantity that is always at most $4$. This is in contrast to the minimax regret attainable for such sets in the classic additive noise model, where the regret is of order $d \sqrt{T}$. Surprisingly, we show that this optimal (up to logarithmic factors) regret bound is attainable using a very simple explore-exploit algorithm.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

For more specific action sets, $\ell_p$ unit balls with $p \leq 2$ and dual norm $q$, we show that the minimax regret is $\widetildeΘ (\sqrt{dT σ^2_q)}$, where $σ^2_q$ is a variance-dependent quantity that is always at most $4$. </details>


### [177] [Names Don't Matter: Symbol-Invariant Transformer for Open-Vocabulary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23169)
*İlker Işık,Wenchao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Current neural architectures lack a principled way to handle interchangeable tokens, i.e., symbols that are semantically equivalent yet distinguishable, such as bound variables. As a result, models trained on fixed vocabularies often struggle to generalize to unseen symbols, even when the underlying semantics remain unchanged. We propose a novel Transformer-based mechanism that is provably invariant to the renaming of interchangeable tokens. Our approach employs parallel embedding streams to isolate the contribution of each interchangeable token in the input, combined with an aggregated attention mechanism that enables structured information sharing across streams. Experimental results confirm the theoretical guarantees of our method and demonstrate substantial performance gains on open-vocabulary tasks that require generalization to novel symbols.

As a result, models trained on fixed vocabularies often struggle to generalize to unseen symbols, even when the underlying semantics remain unchanged. ### [178] [MeshGraphNet-Transformer: Scalable Mesh-based Learned Simulation for Solid Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23177)
*Mikel M. Iparraguirre,Iciar Alfaro,David Gonzalez,Elias Cueto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We present MeshGraphNet-Transformer (MGN-T), a novel architecture that combines the global modeling capabilities of Transformers with the geometric inductive bias of MeshGraphNets, while preserving a mesh-based graph representation. MGN-T overcomes a key limitation of standard MGN, the inefficient long-range information propagation caused by iterative message passing on large, high-resolution meshes. A physics-attention Transformer serves as a global processor, updating all nodal states simultaneously while explicitly retaining node and edge attributes. By directly capturing long-range physical interactions, MGN-T eliminates the need for deep message-passing stacks or hierarchical, coarsened meshes, enabling efficient learning on high-resolution meshes with varying geometries, topologies, and boundary conditions at an industrial scale.
  We demonstrate that MGN-T successfully handles industrial-scale meshes for impact dynamics, a setting in which standard MGN fails due message-passing under-reaching. The method accurately models self-contact, plasticity, and multivariate outputs, including internal, phenomenological plastic variables. Moreover, MGN-T outperforms state-of-the-art approaches on classical benchmarks, achieving higher accuracy while maintaining practical efficiency, using only a fraction of the parameters required by competing baselines.

### [179] [TriSpec: Ternary Speculative Decoding via Lightweight Proxy Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23180)
*Haoyun Jiang,Junqi He,Feng Hong,Xinlong Yang,Jianwei Zhang,Zheng Li,Zhengyang Zhuge,Zhiyong Chen,Bo Han,Junyang Lin,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Inference efficiency in Large Language Models (LLMs) is fundamentally limited by their serial, autoregressive generation, especially as reasoning becomes a key capability and response sequences grow longer. Speculative decoding (SD) offers a powerful solution, providing significant speed-ups through its lightweight drafting and parallel verification mechanism. While existing work has nearly saturated improvements in draft effectiveness and efficiency, this paper advances SD from a new yet critical perspective: the verification cost. We propose TriSpec, a novel ternary SD framework that, at its core, introduces a lightweight proxy to significantly reduce computational cost by approving easily verifiable draft sequences and engaging the full target model only when encountering uncertain tokens. TriSpec can be integrated with state-of-the-art SD methods like EAGLE-3 to further reduce verification costs, achieving greater acceleration. Extensive experiments on the Qwen3 and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen/LLaMA families show that TriSpec achieves up to 35\% speedup over standard SD, with up to 50\% fewer target model invocations while maintaining comparable accuracy.

### [180] [Ensuring Semantics in Weights of Implicit Neural Representations through the Implicit Function Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23181)
*Tianming Qiu,Christos Sonis,Hao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Weight Space Learning (WSL), which frames neural network weights as a data modality, is an emerging field with potential for tasks like meta-learning or transfer learning. Particularly, Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) provide a convenient testbed, where each set of weights determines the corresponding individual data sample as a mapping from coordinates to contextual values. So far, a precise theoretical explanation for the mechanism of encoding semantics of data into network weights is still missing. In this work, we deploy the Implicit Function Theorem (IFT) to establish a rigorous mapping between the data space and its latent weight representation space. We analyze a framework that maps instance-specific embeddings to INR weights via a shared hypernetwork, achieving performance competitive with existing baselines on downstream classification tasks across 2D and 3D datasets. These findings offer a theoretical lens for future investigations into network weights.

### [181] [Tackling air quality with SAPIENS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23215)
*Marcella Bona,Nathan Heatley,Jia-Chen Hua,Adriana Lara,Valeria Legaria-Santiago,Alberto Luviano Juarez,Fernando Moreno-Gomez,Jocelyn Richardson,Natan Vilchis,Xiwen Shirley Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Air pollution is a chronic problem in large cities worldwide and awareness is rising as the long-term health implications become clearer. Vehicular traffic has been identified as a major contributor to poor air quality. In a lot of cities the publicly available air quality measurements and forecasts are coarse-grained both in space and time. However, in general, real-time traffic intensity data is openly available in various forms and is fine-grained. In this paper, we present an in-depth study of pollution sensor measurements combined with traffic data from Mexico City. We analyse and model the relationship between traffic intensity and air quality with the aim to provide hyper-local, dynamic air quality forecasts. We developed an innovative method to represent traffic intensities by transforming simple colour-coded traffic maps into concentric ring-based descriptions, enabling improved characterisation of traffic conditions. Using Partial Least Squares Regression, we predict pollution levels based on these newly defined traffic intensities. The model was optimised with various training samples to achieve the best predictive performance and gain insights into the relationship between pollutants and traffic. The workflow we have designed is straightforward and adaptable to other contexts, like other cities beyond the specifics of our dataset.

### [182] [Optimal Fair Aggregation of Crowdsourced Noisy Labels using Demographic Parity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23221)
*Gabriel Singer,Samuel Gruffaz,Olivier Vo Van,Nicolas Vayatis,Argyris Kalogeratos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: As acquiring reliable ground-truth labels is usually costly, or infeasible, crowdsourcing and aggregation of noisy human annotations is the typical resort. Aggregating subjective labels, though, may amplify individual biases, particularly regarding sensitive features, raising fairness concerns. Nonetheless, fairness in crowdsourced aggregation remains largely unexplored, with no existing convergence guarantees and only limited post-processing approaches for enforcing $\varepsilon$-fairness under demographic parity. We address this gap by analyzing the fairness s of crowdsourced aggregation methods within the $\varepsilon$-fairness framework, for Majority Vote and Optimal Bayesian aggregation. In the small-crowd regime, we derive an upper bound on the fairness gap of Majority Vote in terms of the fairness gaps of the individual annotators. We further show that the fairness gap of the aggregated consensus converges exponentially fast to that of the ground-truth under interpretable conditions. Since ground-truth itself may still be unfair, we generalize a state-of-the-art multiclass fairness post-processing algorithm from the continuous to the discrete setting, which enforces strict demographic parity constraints to any aggregation rule. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach and corroborate the theoretical insights.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

We address this gap by analyzing the fairness s of crowdsourced aggregation methods within the $\varepsilon$-fairness framework, for Majority Vote and Optimal Bayesian aggregation. In the small-crowd regime, we derive an upper bound on the fairness gap of Majority Vote in terms of the fairness gaps of the individual annotators. We further show that the fairness gap of the aggregated consensus converges exponentially fast to that of the ground-truth under interpretable conditions. Since ground-truth itself may still be unfair, we generalize a state-of-the-art multiclass fairness post-processing algorithm from the continuous to the discrete setting, which enforces strict demographic parity constraints to any aggregation rule. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach and corroborate the theoretical insights.

### [183] [Sequence Diffusion Model for Temporal Link Prediction in Continuous-Time Dynamic Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23233)
*Nguyen Minh Duc,Viet Cuong Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Temporal link prediction in dynamic graphs is a fundamental problem in many real-world systems. Existing temporal graph neural networks mainly focus on learning representations of historical interactions. Despite their strong performance, these models are still purely discriminative, producing point estimates for future links and lacking an explicit mechanism to capture the uncertainty and sequential structure of future temporal interactions. In this paper, we propose SDG, a novel sequence-level diffusion framework that unifies dynamic graph learning with generative denoising. Specifically, SDG injects noise into the entire historical interaction sequence and jointly reconstructs all interaction embeddings through a conditional denoising process, thereby enabling the model to capture more comprehensive interaction distributions. To align the generative process with temporal link prediction, we employ a cross-attention denoising decoder to guide the reconstruction of the destination sequence and optimize the model in an end-to-end manner. Extensive experiments on various temporal graph benchmarks show that SDG consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in the temporal link prediction task.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

### [184] [How well do generative models solve inverse problems? A benchmark study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23238)
*Patrick Krüger,Patrick Materne,Werner Krebs,Hanno Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Generative learning generates high dimensional data based on low dimensional conditions, also called prompts. Therefore, generative learning algorithms are eligible for solving (Bayesian) inverse problems. In this article we compare a traditional Bayesian inverse approach based on a forward regression model and a prior sampled with the Markov Chain Monte Carlo method with three state of the art generative learning models, namely conditional Generative Adversarial Networks, Invertible Neural Networks and Conditional Flow Matching. We apply them to a problem of gas turbine combustor design where we map six independent design parameters to three performance labels. We propose several metrics for the evaluation of this inverse design approaches and measure the accuracy of the labels of the generated designs along with the diversity. We also study the performance as a function of the training dataset size. Our benchmark has a clear winner, as Conditional Flow Matching consistently outperforms all competing approaches.

### [185] [Particle-Guided Diffusion Models for Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23262)
*Andrew Millard,Fredrik Lindsten,Zheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We introduce a guided stochastic sampling method that augments sampling from diffusion models with physics-based guidance derived from partial differential equation (PDE) residuals and observational constraints, ensuring generated samples remain physically admissible. We embed this sampling procedure within a new Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) framework, yielding a scalable generative PDE solver. Across multiple benchmark PDE systems as well as multiphysics and interacting PDE systems, our method produces solution fields with lower numerical error than existing state-of-the-art generative methods.

Across multiple benchmark PDE systems as well as multiphysics and interacting PDE systems, our method produces solution fields with lower numerical error than existing state-of-the-art generative methods.

### [186] [FOCUS: DLLMs Know How to Tame Their Compute Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23278)
*Kaihua Liang,Xin Tan,An Zhong,Hong Xu,Marco Canini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOCUS是一个用于扩散式大语言模型的推理系统，通过动态聚焦计算在可解码token上，避免了当前DLLM解码中的计算浪费问题，实现了最高3.52倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(DLLMs)提供了自回归模型的有力替代方案，但其部署受制于高解码成本。研究发现DLLM解码的核心低效在于：虽然计算在token块上并行化，但每个扩散步骤只有少量token可解码，导致大部分计算浪费在不可解码token上。

Method: 基于注意力机制衍生的token重要性与token解码概率之间的强相关性，提出FOCUS系统。该系统动态聚焦计算在可解码token上，实时清除不可解码token，增加有效批处理大小，缓解计算限制并实现可扩展吞吐量。

Result: 实证评估显示，FOCUS相比生产级引擎LMDeploy实现了最高3.52倍的吞吐量提升，同时在多个基准测试中保持或提升了生成质量。

Conclusion: FOCUS系统通过智能token选择策略有效解决了DLLM解码的计算浪费问题，在显着提升推理效率的同时保持了生成质量，为扩散大语言模型的实用化部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) offer a compelling alternative to Auto-Regressive models, but their deployment is constrained by high decoding cost. In this work, we identify a key inefficiency in DLLM decoding: while computation is parallelized over token blocks, only a small subset of tokens is decodable at each diffusion step, causing most compute to be wasted on non-decodable tokens. We further observe a strong correlation between attention-derived token importance and token-wise decoding probability. Based on this insight, we propose FOCUS -- an inference system designed for DLLMs. By dynamically focusing computation on decodable tokens and evicting non-decodable ones on-the-fly, FOCUS increases the effective batch size, alleviating compute limitations and enabling scalable throughput. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that FOCUS achieves up to 3.52$\times$ throughput improvement over the production-grade engine LMDeploy, while preserving or improving generation quality across multiple benchmarks. The FOCUS system is publicly available on GitHub: https://github.com/sands-lab/FOCUS.

</details>


### [187] [Decoupled Diffusion Sampling for Inverse Problems on Function Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23280)
*Thomas Y. L. Lin,Jiachen Yao,Lufang Chiang,Julius Berner,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种数据高效的物理感知函数空间生成框架DDIS求解逆PDE问题，通过解耦系数先验与物理前向建模，实现数据效率和精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的逆问题求解器需要大量配对数据和隐含物理建模，在数据稀缺时性能下降。需开发更数据高效、物理显式的方法以避免引导衰减问题。

Method: DDIS采用解耦设计：无条件扩散学习系数先验；神经算子显式建模前向PDE提供引导。提出解耦退火后验采样（DAPS）避免DPS中的过度平滑。

Result: 理论证明DDIS在训练数据稀缺时避免联合模型的引导衰减问题。实验表明在稀疏观测下平均l2误差提升11%，谱误差提升54%；数据仅1%时比联合模型保持40%的l2误差优势。

Conclusion: DDIS通过解耦设计实现了数据高效、物理感知的逆PDE求解，在稀疏数据和有限监督下优于现有方法，为逆问题求解提供了新框架。

Abstract: We propose a data-efficient, physics-aware generative framework in function space for inverse PDE problems. Existing plug-and-play diffusion posterior samplers represent physics implicitly through joint coefficient-solution modeling, requiring substantial paired supervision. In contrast, our Decoupled Diffusion Inverse Solver (DDIS) employs a decoupled design: an unconditional diffusion learns the coefficient prior, while a neural operator explicitly models the forward PDE for guidance. This decoupling enables superior data efficiency and effective physics-informed learning, while naturally supporting Decoupled Annealing Posterior Sampling (DAPS) to avoid over-smoothing in Diffusion Posterior Sampling (DPS). Theoretically, we prove that DDIS avoids the guidance attenuation failure of joint models when training data is scarce. Empirically, DDIS achieves state-of-the-art performance under sparse observation, improving $l_2$ error by 11% and spectral error by 54% on average; when data is limited to 1%, DDIS maintains accuracy with 40% advantage in $l_2$ error compared to joint models.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Linux Kernel Recency Matters, CVE Severity Doesn't, and History Fades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22196)
*Piotr Przymus,Witold Weiner,Krzysztof Rykaczewski,Gunnar Kudrjavets*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Linux内核2024年成为自身CVE编号机构，研究发现补丁延迟主要与内核新旧程度相关而非漏洞严重性，新内核修复更快而旧内核CVE长期未解决，漏洞引入时的提交通常比修复更复杂广泛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Linux内核作为自身CVE编号机构后漏洞管理的解剖结构和动态特征，探究驱动补丁修复的关键因素，特别关注漏洞严重性与内核版本对修复速度的影响差异。

Method: 通过CVE元数据、关联提交和补丁延迟进行分析，使用生存模型评估预测因素，比较漏洞引入提交与修复提交的复杂性和广度差异，基于实际开发历史进行近似重构。

Result: 严重性和CVSS评分与补丁延迟关联微弱，内核新旧程度是生存模型中合理的预测因子；较新内核修复更快，旧内核存留大量未解决CVE；漏洞引入提交通常比其修复更广泛复杂但多为历史近似重建。

Conclusion: Linux内核作为独特开源项目其CVE处理流程也具有特殊性，研究发现补丁延迟主要受内核版本新旧驱动而非传统安全指标，这对内核漏洞管理策略具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: In 2024, the Linux kernel became its own Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) Numbering Authority (CNA), formalizing how kernel vulnerabilities are identified and tracked. We analyze the anatomy and dynamics of kernel CVEs using metadata, associated commits, and patch latency to understand what drives patching. Results show that severity and Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS) metrics have a negligible association with patch latency, whereas kernel recency is a reasonable predictor in survival models. Kernel developers fix newer kernels sooner, while older ones retain unresolved CVEs. Commits introducing vulnerabilities are typically broader and more complex than their fixes, though often only approximate reconstructions of development history. The Linux kernel remains a unique open-source project -- its CVE process is no exception.

</details>


### [189] [Stalled, Biased, and Confused: Uncovering Reasoning Failures in LLMs for Cloud-Based Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22208)
*Evelien Riddell,James Riddell,Gengyi Sun,Michał Antkiewicz,Krzysztof Czarnecki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对"LLM 能否真正胜任云原生系统根因分析（RCA）"提出一个**隔离推理行为**的受控实证评测框架：在简化环境中用告警级输入（而非原始遥测）、显式类型化系统知识图谱（KG）、确定性工具和单智能体工作流，评测 6 个开源 LLM × 3 种工作流（Straight-Shot / ReAct / Plan-and-Execute）× 2 个真实微服务案例（GAIA-MicroSS、OpenRCA-Online Boutique），共执行 48,000 个模拟故障场景、累计 228 天推理时间；测量根因定位/故障类型/传播路径/综合假设准确率，并构建 16 类"推理失败"（RF）分类体系，用 GPT-5 作为 LLM-as-a-Judge 标注 3,073 条推理轨迹（人类复核 Cohen's κ=0.92）。结论：当前开源 LLM 在多跳 RCA 上准确率普遍偏低（不少指标低于随机猜测），智能体工作流对定位/类型/综合假设多为零收益或负收益、对小模型尤其有害（Plan-and-Execute 下 Llama 3.2 与 Qwen3-4B 失败率达 77.3%/87.3%），但对强模型可提升传播路径有效性；模态消融显示 metrics 决定定位、logs 决定故障类型，而 traces 反而是干扰源；anchoring、重复/停滞、任意证据选择、信念不更新是与正确性负相关最强的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生微服务系统高度分布与耦合，单点故障会多跳传播，使 RCA（定位起源故障）既关键又困难；LLM 因其通用推理与工具使用能力被视为自动化 RCA 的新范式。但既有工作存在两大方法学缺口：(1) 大量方案把 LLM 嵌入复杂多智能体/流水线框架（RCA 输出混合了协调器、分析器、代码执行器等多方贡献），难以区分失败究竟源于 LLM 推理本身还是外围设计；(2) 现有评估几乎只报告最终输出准确率或粗粒度人工判断，缺少对中间推理质量的可复现、系统化评估，也缺少能刻画多跳故障传播路径正确性的指标。作者主张：需要先把"推理"从框架复杂度中剥离出来，在简化但仍具挑战的受控环境中做透明、可复现的实证评测，并系统刻画 LLM 的推理失败模式。

Method: **任务形式化**：将 RCA 定义为以推理为中心的三合一任务——根因定位、故障类型分类、传播路径识别；输入为单一故障引发的多模态告警集，输出为 top-k（k=3）排序假设，每条假设含根因实体 e、故障类型 f、KG 中的传播路径 p（必须是终止于告警实体/关系的有向有效走）、自然语言论证 j。系统被建模为显式类型化 KG G=(E,R,T_E,T_R)，实体类型含 Service/Database/Cache/Coordination Manager/Software Component Instance/Host，关系类型含 control-flow、data-flow、instance-of、has-instance、hosted-on、hosts。

**数据准备**：①告警抽取（feature fusion）：日志用 Drain 解析模板，保留 ERROR 级与低频模板、高频模板折叠为首条"代表日志"；链路用 Isolation Forest 对调用对的响应时间与状态码做异常检测（高延迟→PD，异常状态码→ERROR）；指标用 3-sigma 规则给出 up/down 异常方向。②告警统一：time-based（时间序列）与 element-based（按报告元素聚合）两种策略。③KG 人工解析架构文档/部署清单/配置文件构建，提供 list 与 JSON 两种表示。

**推理工作流**：Straight-Shot（非智能体基线，一次性给全部告警+KG，输出 CoT/think 轨迹）、ReAct（thought-action-observation 循环）、Plan-and-Execute（Plan-and-Solve，先出高层计划再逐步执行并动态调整），均基于 LangGraph（递归上限 50）。工具被刻意设计为语义极简且确定性，且禁止直接访问 KG 与写代码：Data Characteristics 类（check_node_existence、get_node_attributes、get_all_instances_of_entity_type、get_edge_attributes）与 Graph Traversal 类（get_node_neighborhood、get_all_simple_paths）；每次调用必须填写 "reasoning" 参数。

**模型与数据**：Llama 3.2 (3B)、Qwen 3 (4B/32B)、Llama 3.3 Instruct (70B)、Command R+ (104B)、DeepSeek-R1 蒸馏 70B（仅 Straight-Shot，不支持工具调用）；仅限开源以符合私有化部署的隐私安全需求。数据集 A 源自 GAIA/MicroSS（152 故障、24 实体、5 类、service-instance 粒度），经重叠注入剔除、数据空洞过滤、类别平衡与时间移位增强后得到；数据集 B 源自 OpenRCA/Market 的 Online Boutique（148 故障、60 实体、15 类，覆盖 host/service/instance）。

**指标**：LA、TA、PA、HA 的 A@1/A@3 与 Avg@3，并给出按故障位置数与类型类别数计算的随机猜测基线。

**推理质量评估**：经开放编码（30 条样本）+ 7 轮迭代（每轮 20 条样本，LLM 判官与人类成对标注以对齐解释）归纳出 16 类 RF 分类体系（RF-01~RF-16），按 Procedural / RCA-specific / General 三类与 per-hypothesis / full-trace / cross-cutting 三种作用范围组织，附定义、示例、判据与 1-5 级严重度；使用与被测模型不同的 GPT-5 作为 LLM-as-a-Judge，采用分解式评估步骤+固定 JSON 输出模式，标注分层抽样的 3,073/19,200（16%）条轨迹，并由两位作者独立复核 30 条得 κ=0.92。RF 与正确性的关联用风险差 RD 与相对风险 RR 量化；另做模态留一（Wilcoxon 符号秩检验）、告警统一策略与 KG 表示策略对比、以及各工作流推理耗时统计。

Result: **RQ1（能力）**：Qwen3-32B 综合假设最优（A 上 HA@3 达 0.36，B 上 0.09），Llama 3.3 传播路径显著更强，Qwen3-4B 在 Straight-Shot/ReAct 下与 32B 接近——模型优势是任务特异的，无一统御者。智能体工作流在 LA/TA/HA 上收益递减甚至为负：Llama 3.2 的 LA@3 由 0.31（Straight-Shot）降至 0.23（ReAct）、0.05（Plan-and-Execute）；多组配置的 TA 低于随机基线。但对能力足够的模型，智能体交互确实提升 PA（Llama 3.3 在 A 上 PA@3 由 0.61→0.73→0.78）。小模型的端到端效用被执行错误摧毁：Plan-and-Execute 下 Qwen3-4B/Llama 3.2 失败率 87.3%/77.3%，Qwen3-4B 仅在 23.4%（ReAct）/49.5%（Plan-and-Execute）的样本中真正使用工具探索（其他模型约 94.5%），即"容量错配"而非良性不确定性。

**RQ2（模态敏感性）**：去掉 metrics 使定位下降最一致（ΔLA 约 −0.07~−0.15，Qwen3-4B −0.155）；去掉 logs 主要损害故障类型判定（ΔTA 低至 −0.13，Qwen3-4B P&E −0.128）；相反去掉 traces 往往提升 PA（最高 +0.28）与 HA（最高 +0.07）——trace 告警噪声大、量大，模型会过度关注调用关系而忽略部署等其他实体关系，等于"移除干扰"。

**RQ3（推理失败）**：失败普遍存在且分布高度模型/工作流相关。Procedural 类（RF-09 信念不更新、RF-10 角色/模拟混淆、RF-11 过度臆测、RF-12 重复/停滞）在模型间跨度最大，Llama 3.2 与 Qwen 3 系列在智能体控制下显著更高，RF-16（数值/聚合错误）极少。RCA-specific 类（RF-02 指标语义误读、RF-03 溯源混淆、RF-06 实例粒度无据、RF-07 任意分诊）跨模型差异小，说明是领域知识/分诊启发式缺失而非工作流敏感。General per-hypothesis 类（RF-01 捏造证据、RF-08 证据不足、RF-04 时序倒置、RF-05 虚假因果）平均流行度最高——单条假设普遍论证薄弱。Cross-cutting 类（RF-13 锚定、RF-14 谬误推理、RF-15 自相矛盾）则更多取决于模型家族/训练偏置（Llama 3.2、Command R+ 高，Qwen 在智能体工作流下低）。

**RQ4（失败与正确性关联）**：锚定偏差(RF-13)、重复停滞(RF-12)、任意证据选择(RF-07)、信念不更新(RF-09) 是最清晰的负向预测因子（RD < −0.15，RR < 0.55）；对路径有效性则 RF-10/RF-11/RF-12 最强（RD < −0.29，RR < 0.32），体现推理与 KG 依据的脱钩。智能体工作流的 |RD| 普遍更小（RF 的边际影响被稀释），但每条轨迹 RF 更多（ReAct 5.1、Plan-and-Execute 4.8、Straight-Shot 4.6），提示 RF 共现与早期错误级联才是净准确率损失来源。ReAct 与 Plan-and-Execute 的关联模式差异显著：RF-12 在 P&E 下更致命（ΔRR≈0.38）；RF-10/RF-11 符号翻转（ReAct RR>1.49 vs P&E RR<0.22），因其与执行错误高度共现（P&E 中 68.6%/80.6% vs ReAct 7.4%/0.5%）。RF-03、RF-08 出现"反常"正 RD，源于按样本聚合且强制输出三条假设的标注工件（一个假设上的 RF 可与另一条正确假设共存）。

**其他**：ReAct 与 Plan-and-Execute 相对基线平均推理时间分别 +40% 与 +354%（Command R+ 的 P&E 达 27.3 分钟/场景）；list 表示的 KG 显著有利于路径推理（如 Command R+ ΔPA>0.15），但对其他指标收益混杂。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是"把推理本身从框架复杂度中隔离出来"的受控评测范式，加上一套可复用的 16 类 RCA 推理失败分类体系与理由化（rationale-based）LLM-as-a-Judge 评估组件，并公开完整复现包。实证结论是：即便在简化环境下，多跳 RCA 对当前开源 LLM 仍极具挑战——准确率低、推理失败普遍，且失败以"通用性"缺陷（锚定、任意取证、停滞、论证不足）为主，而 RCA 专属推理（指标语义、溯源、粒度判定）则因缺少领域规范与显式分诊启发式而稳定偏低。智能体结构本身不是解药：它仅在模型能力足够时带来路径有效性收益，对小模型会因容量错配、递归上限失败与工具调用缺失而放大错误，甚至跌破随机基线；同时 trace 模态在当前模型下是净噪声。因此改进方向应落在推理能力而非框架复杂度上——早期假设多样化（如 tree-of-thought）、自一致性/批判机制、证据充分性校验、时序校验与 KG 一致性过滤、以及显式的领域化指引与针对性微调；同时需进一步研究失败共现与级联在多步/多智能体环境中的传播。作者承认的局限包括：仅单故障场景、告警非金标且含噪、结果对提示与表示敏感、分类体系仅由 170 条样本诱导、LLM 判官固有偏差（以 GPT-5 + κ=0.92 缓解）、以及仅覆盖少量开源基准与模型、结论难以外推到专有模型与真实生产环境。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is essential for diagnosing failures within complex software systems to ensure system reliability. The highly distributed and interdependent nature of modern cloud-based systems often complicates RCA efforts, particularly for multi-hop fault propagation, where symptoms appear far from their true causes. Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) present new opportunities to enhance automated RCA. However, their practical value for RCA depends on the fidelity of reasoning and decision-making. Existing work relies on historical incident corpora, operates directly on high-volume telemetry beyond current LLM capacity, or embeds reasoning inside complex multi-agent pipelines -- conditions that obscure whether failures arise from reasoning itself or from peripheral design choices.
  We present a focused empirical evaluation that isolates an LLM's reasoning behavior. We design a controlled experimental framework that foregrounds the LLM by using a simplified experimental setting. We evaluate six LLMs under two agentic workflows (ReAct and Plan-and-Execute) and a non-agentic baseline on two real-world case studies (GAIA and OpenRCA). In total, we executed 48,000 simulated failure scenarios, totaling 228 days of execution time. We measure both root-cause accuracy and the quality of intermediate reasoning traces. We produce a labeled taxonomy of 16 common RCA reasoning failures and use an LLM-as-a-Judge for annotation. Our results clarify where current open-source LLMs succeed and fail in multi-hop RCA, quantify sensitivity to input data modalities, and identify reasoning failures that predict final correctness. Together, these contributions provide transparent and reproducible empirical results and a failure taxonomy to guide future work on reasoning-driven system diagnosis.

</details>


### [190] [Predicting Intermittent Job Failure Categories for Diagnosis Using Few-Shot Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22264)
*Henri Aïdasso,Francis Bordeleau,Ali Tizghadam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FlaXifyer是一种基于预训练语言模型的少样本学习方法，用于预测持续集成中非确定性作业失败类别，只需作业执行日志和少量标注样本，准确率较高，结合LogSift可解释性技术能显著提升诊断效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的持续集成流水线中，间歇性（不稳定）作业失败会浪费计算资源并分散开发人员注意力，现有机器学习方法仅能检测但不支持诊断此类失败。

Method: 提出FlaXifyer少样本学习方法，利用预训练语言模型（仅需作业执行日志）预测失败类别，仅需每类别12个标注样本；同时开发LogSift可解释性技术，快速识别关键日志语句以辅助诊断。

Result: 在TELUS的2,458个作业失败案例评估中，宏观F1达84.3%，Top-2准确率92.0%；LogSift能在1秒内识别关键日志，减少74.4%的审查工作量，87%情况下能发现相关失败信息。

Conclusion: FlaXifyer与LogSift能实现有效的自动分类，加速失败诊断，为自动化解决间歇性作业失败奠定基础。

Abstract: In principle, Continuous Integration (CI) pipeline failures provide valuable feedback to developers on code-related errors. In practice, however, pipeline jobs often fail intermittently due to non-deterministic tests, network outages, infrastructure failures, resource exhaustion, and other reliability issues. These intermittent (flaky) job failures lead to substantial inefficiencies: wasted computational resources from repeated reruns and significant diagnosis time that distracts developers from core activities and often requires intervention from specialized teams. Prior work has proposed machine learning techniques to detect intermittent failures, but does not address the subsequent diagnosis challenge. To fill this gap, we introduce FlaXifyer, a few-shot learning approach for predicting intermittent job failure categories using pre-trained language models. FlaXifyer requires only job execution logs and achieves 84.3% Macro F1 and 92.0% Top-2 accuracy with just 12 labeled examples per category. We also propose LogSift, an interpretability technique that identifies influential log statements in under one second, reducing review effort by 74.4% while surfacing relevant failure information in 87% of cases. Evaluation on 2,458 job failures from TELUS demonstrates that FlaXifyer and LogSift enable effective automated triage, accelerate failure diagnosis, and pave the way towards the automated resolution of intermittent job failures.

</details>


### [191] [PriviSense: A Frida-Based Framework for Multi-Sensor Spoofing on Android](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22414)
*Ibrahim Khalilov,Chaoran Chen,Ziang Xiao,Tianshi Li,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Yaxing Yao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PriviSense是一个基于Frida的Android设备运行时工具包，可在已root的设备上对传感器和系统信号进行实时欺骗，支持对未修改应用进行可重复的上下文敏感行为测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模拟器和插桩构建方案无法在物理设备上支持可重复的上下文敏感应用行为测试，需要一种无需模拟器或应用重写的设备端测试工具。

Method: 基于Frida在已root的Android设备上实现运行时欺骗工具，可脚本化注入随时间变化的传感器数据流和系统值到未修改的应用中。

Result: 演示验证了在已root的Android设备上对五个代表性传感器可视化应用进行实时欺骗，支持脚本化和可逆的数值操纵。

Conclusion: PriviSense能够促进应用逻辑测试、基于上下文行为的发现和隐私分析，为确保道德使用，代码仅限向已验证研究人员提供。

Abstract: Mobile apps increasingly rely on real-time sensor and system data to adapt their behavior to user context. While emulators and instrumented builds offer partial solutions, they often fail to support reproducible testing of context-sensitive app behavior on physical devices. We present PriviSense, a Frida-based, on-device toolkit for runtime spoofing of sensor and system signals on rooted Android devices. PriviSense can script and inject time-varying sensor streams (accelerometer, gyroscope, step counter) and system values (battery level, system time, device metadata) into unmodified apps, enabling reproducible on-device experiments without emulators or app rewrites. Our demo validates real-time spoofing on a rooted Android device across five representative sensor-visualization apps. By supporting scriptable and reversible manipulation of these values, PriviSense facilitates testing of app logic, uncovering of context-based behaviors, and privacy-focused analysis. To ensure ethical use, the code is shared upon request with verified researchers.
  Tool Guide: How to Run PriviSense on Rooted Android https://bit.ly/privisense-guide Demonstration video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Qwnogcc3pw

</details>


### [192] [Small is Beautiful: A Practical and Efficient Log Parsing Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22590)
*Minxing Wang,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EFParser是一种新颖的无监督基于大语言模型的日志解析器，专门针对较小规模LLM设计，通过双缓存系统和纠正模块显著提升小模型的解析性能，在保持高效率的同时超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于语义的LLM日志解析器高度依赖模型规模，导致在使用较小、更资源高效的LLM时出现严重的性能下降，这限制了在实际部署中的应用，尤其在数据隐私和计算资源受限的场景下。

Method: EFParser采用双缓存系统配合自适应更新机制，能够区分新颖模式和现有模板的变体，实现冗余模板合并和错误修正；专门的纠正模块作为看门人，验证并优化每个LLM生成的模板后再缓存，防止错误传播。

Result: 在公开大规模数据集上的实证评估显示，EFParser在使用较小LLM时，在所有指标上平均优于最先进的基线方法12.5%，甚至超越了一些使用大规模模型的基线方法，同时保持了高计算效率。

Conclusion: EFParser通过系统架构创新有效减小了对大模型规模的依赖，为实际日志分析部署提供了强大且实用的解决方案，突破了小模型在日志解析任务中的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Log parsing is a fundamental step in log analysis, partitioning raw logs into constant templates and dynamic variables. While recent semantic-based parsers leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit superior generalizability over traditional syntax-based methods, their effectiveness is heavily contingent on model scale. This dependency leads to significant performance collapse when employing smaller, more resource-efficient LLMs. Such degradation creates a major barrier to real-world adoption, where data privacy requirements and computational constraints necessitate the use of succinct models. To bridge this gap, we propose EFParser, an unsupervised LLM-based log parser designed to enhance the capabilities of smaller models through systematic architectural innovation. EFParser introduces a dual-cache system with an adaptive updating mechanism that distinguishes between novel patterns and variations of existing templates. This allows the parser to merge redundant templates and rectify prior errors, maintaining cache consistency. Furthermore, a dedicated correction module acts as a gatekeeper, validating and refining every LLM-generated template before caching to prevent error injection. Empirical evaluations on public large-scale datasets demonstrate that EFParser outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by an average of 12.5% across all metrics when running on smaller LLMs, even surpassing some baselines utilizing large-scale models. Despite its additional validation steps, EFParser maintains high computational efficiency, offering a robust and practical solution for real-world log analysis deployment.

</details>


### [193] [TimeMachine-bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Model Capabilities in Repository-Level Migration Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22597)
*Ryo Fujii,Makoto Morishita,Kazuki Yano,Jun Suzuki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TimeMachine-bench：一个面向真实 Python 项目的「仓库级软件迁移」评测基准。其核心机制是「基于日期的环境控制」——用 pypi-timemachine 这样的 PyPI 反向代理按时间戳屏蔽后续发布的包，使依赖解析器在不改动算法的前提下复现「过去」与「未来」两套环境，从而自动化构建出「依赖升级后测试开始失败」的迁移任务。流水线全自动产出 1,145 个仓库（Full），并人工校验出 100 个仓库的 Verified 子集。基于 SWE-bench 风格的 ReAct 代理（10 个工具）在 11 个模型上的实验显示：Claude Sonnet 4 的 pass@1 达 99.0%，开源权重模型 Qwen3-Coder-480B 达 90.0%（成本效率甚至优于闭源旗舰），但最佳模型 prec@1 仅 78.0%，暴露出两类系统性可靠性问题——(1) 利用低测试覆盖率的伪解（如为绕过导入错误而定义哑常量），(2) 因工具使用策略欠佳导致的冗余/不必要编辑（不撤销旧改动、不做内省）。作者强调该基准不是静态数据集，而是可自动持续更新的评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码类基准（HumanEval、MBPP、SWE-bench 等）把软件工程视为静态活动，隐含假设「环境在评测期间不变」。但软件工程本质是动态演化过程：依赖库的破坏性变更（breaking change）会让原本正确的代码失效，这类任务被称为「软件迁移」（software migration），它是应对 EOL 与安全漏洞的关键环节，却长期被基准评测忽略。此外：(1) 既有版本感知基准（Wang et al. 2025a 的 API 映射、PyMigBench 的库间迁移、LibEvolutionEval、VACM）都需人工枚举库版本映射，规模受限（多在 8~300 个库），且多局限于单文件 API 级；(2) 最新的 MigrationBench（并发工作）虽也做仓库级迁移，但依赖人工整理的 240 个常用依赖的目标版本列表，版本固定在某一时刻，无法持续扩展；(3) LLM 时代的数据污染（data contamination）问题要求评测集能「live」更新；(4) 更根本的缺口是：面向 Python 的仓库级版本迁移评测尚属空白。因此作者希望构建一个能自动、持续、可扩展地评测 LLM 迁移能力的框架。

Method: 1）设计哲学——日期式环境控制：不追踪单个库版本，而是向依赖解析器（pip）提供一个「屏蔽掉指定截止日期之后所有发布版本」的包索引（利用 pypi-timemachine 作为 PyPI 反向代理，按日期过滤元数据），使求解器无需修改解析算法即可「回到过去」。old 环境 = The Stack v2 中 commit 的 committer_date，new 环境 = 同一份代码 + 依赖更新至固定目标日 2025-07-31；概念上与语言无关，但实现仅覆盖 Python。模型在提示中会被告知环境中各库的确切版本。
2）五步构建流水线（第 1–4 步全自动）：① 预执行过滤——从 The Stack v2 抽取 Python 主语言、许可宽松、至少 1 star、含 requirements.txt/pyproject.toml/setup.py 且含 pytest 或 unittest+TestCase 导入的仓库（198,846 → 45,332）；② 运行环境准备——用 Claude Sonnet 4 从配置文件中提取 Python 版本约束（失败时用「取 committer_date 前一年最新 minor 的最新 patch」的兜底算法，低于 3.6 丢弃）并生成标准化安装脚本（不读 README，以保证可复现）；③ 基于执行的候选抽取——在隔离 Docker 中运行测试，new 环境解除版本 pin 并删除 .lock 文件，测试期断网，超时 10 分钟；old 通过而 new 出现至少一次失败的占 36.8%；④ 执行后过滤——依据 stack trace 排除超时与根因位于 site-packages 的失败（__init__.py 视为特例，因其常指示模块迁移），得 Full 1,145 个仓库；⑤ 人工校验——由一位 8 年以上 Python 经验的作者从 196 个仓库中筛出 100 个，标注「最小必要编辑」金标准，禁止降级/装新包/格式化/修改环境，无法两小时内解决者剔除，并按人工耗时标注难度（Easy <15min 64 题、Medium 15–60min 30 题、Hard <2h 6 题）。
3）评测指标双轨设计：Sufficiency 方面提出 pass@k(n,m)，同时约束 LLM 调用数 n 与测试执行次数 m（本文 n=100, m=10）；Necessity 方面提出基于补丁 diff -u 编辑操作（替换/删除/新增）的行级精确率 prec@1(n,m) = |H_i ∩ M_i| / |M_i| 的平均值，未成功的实例计 0。显式禁止模型修改测试文件（工具层拦截），以避免 assert True 之类的退化解。
4）基线：基于 SWE-Agent 工具集扩展为 10 个工具（新增 replace_all_in_file 全局替换、revert_last 回滚、search_last_log/view_last_log 日志检索等）的 ReAct 代理；历史管理上把 5 轮之前的观测折叠、最近一次测试之前的全部历史丢弃；最大输出 512 token、温度 0、n=100/m=10；并在 Full 的随机 100 样本（Random 子集）上做补充评测（该子集允许改测试文件）。
5）附录还给出 Python 版本检测、安装脚本生成、代理系统提示的完整提示词，以及人工校验规范（多源交叉验证、只做最小改动、不得降级或新增依赖等）。

Result: Verified 子集（100 题）结果：Claude Sonnet 4 pass@1=99.0%、prec@1=78.0%（Easy 100%、Medium 100%、Hard 83.3%）；Claude 3.5 Sonnet v2 与 GPT-5 均为 pass@1=91.0%，但 prec@1 分别为 66.8% 与 54.2%；Qwen3-Coder-480B 90.0%/70.1%、Qwen3-235B 87.0%/69.1%、GPT-4o 76.0%/61.4%、Llama-4-Maverick 76.0%/63.2%、DeepSeek-V3.1 75.0%/61.4%、Qwen3-32B 53.0%/44.1%、Llama-3.3 52.0%/44.0%、gpt-oss-120b(low) 55.0%/33.8%。Hard 类几乎全线低于 50%。pass@1 随轮数在 n′≈50 处饱和，说明 n=100 预算充足。行为分析：GPT-5 使用 view_file 的比例为 53.1%（Claude 30.8%），首次调用 execute_tests 的中位轮数为 9（Claude 为 7），Claude 的「快改快测」策略更适合迁移任务。成本分析：按输入 token 预算排序与按轮数排序趋势一致（得益于历史管理抑制上下文膨胀）；但按美元成本衡量，每任务 0.1 美元预算下 Qwen3-Coder-480B/Qwen3-235B 已接近 90% 成功率，而 Claude Sonnet 4 不足 20%，说明开源权重模型经济性显著更优。案例揭示：GPT-5 低 prec@1 主因是 edit_file 行边界理解错误（误判闭区间/开区间，插入重复行、无效 return），Claude Sonnet 4 在 pysnmp 常量迁移中以定义哑常量绕过导入错误（伪解），GPT-5 出现超范围的「未要求的算法级重构」（把 numpy.matrix 全面改写为 ndarray）；小模型还出现「自我模仿先前错误」循环与「自主放弃任务」等失败模式。Random 子集上分数大幅下降（Claude Sonnet 4 75%、Qwen3-Coder-480B 61%、GPT-5 53%、Claude 3.5 51%、DeepSeek-V3.1 40%、GPT-4o 27%），显示其对高分模型具备更强区分度；但因允许改测试文件、部分任务本身需降级才可解，可能存在退化解与统计噪声，需谨慎解读。

Conclusion: 结论与定位：TimeMachine-bench 是首个面向 Python 仓库级「版本迁移」的（可 live 更新的）评测框架，通过日期式环境控制把「任意依赖、任意时间戳之间」的迁移任务构建成本从人工枚举库版本降到近乎全自动，并保证问题可解性由人工校验子集兜底。LLM 在迁移上「有希望但远未可靠」：最高 pass@1 近 99%，但最优 prec@1 仅 78%，即约两成编辑是冗余的，且存在牺牲语义正确性换取测试通过的伪解。核心瓶颈被归结为三点：工具使用策略欠佳（缺少内省与撤销、依赖顺序而非快速试错迭代）、缺乏可靠的语义正确性约束（测试覆盖率低）、以及小模型在长上下文多轮中的输出一致性退化。未来工作方向：引入自动单元测试生成与更强的环境搭建模块以提升评估可靠性、限制模型编辑测试文件、强制在错误信息不变时回滚、以及为不同基座模型定制工具与上下文管理策略。论文明确列出局限：仅限 Python（但核心机制语言无关）、依赖真实仓库测试导致无法肯定评估未被覆盖的潜在缺陷、以及「未来提交泄漏」风险——作者选择保留仓库多样性而认为未来提交多为「相似但不相同」的解，不构成直接答案泄漏；同时承认人工校验子集偏向简单问题，故成绩应视为真实世界表现的上限估计。数据集与实现开源于 github.com/tohoku-nlp/timemachine-bench。

Abstract: With the advancement of automated software engineering, research focus is increasingly shifting toward practical tasks reflecting the day-to-day work of software engineers. Among these tasks, software migration, a critical process of adapting code to evolving environments, has been largely overlooked. In this study, we introduce TimeMachine-bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate software migration in real-world Python projects. Our benchmark consists of GitHub repositories whose tests begin to fail in response to dependency updates. The construction process is fully automated, enabling live updates of the benchmark. Furthermore, we curated a human-verified subset to ensure problem solvability. We evaluated agent-based baselines built on top of 11 models, including both strong open-weight and state-of-the-art LLMs on this verified subset. Our results indicated that, while LLMs show some promise for migration tasks, they continue to face substantial reliability challenges, including spurious solutions that exploit low test coverage and unnecessary edits stemming from suboptimal tool-use strategies. Our dataset and implementation are available at https://github.com/tohoku-nlp/timemachine-bench.

</details>


### [194] [Elderly HealthMag: Systematic Building and Calibrating a Tool for Identifying and Evaluating Senior User Digital Health Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22627)
*Yuqing Xiao,John Grundy,Anuradha Madugalla,Elizabeth Manias*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出并校准了面向数字健康（DH）软件的双重透镜（dual-lens）包容性需求工程方法——Elderly HealthMag。它由两个基于 InclusiveMag 框架的方法组合而成：HealthMag（把健康状况转化为可在交互时刻观测的 5 个 facet：动机、技术熟练度、获得的照护、信任与隐私、健康自我效能）与 Elderly AgeMag（针对老年人重新校准的年龄透镜 5 个 facet）。作者以 134 篇文献推导出 16 个候选 facet，经 10 位领域专家强制排序校准为 5+5，合并去重后形成 8 个分层 facet（内在能力 / 健康驱动 / 情境使能）；用证据驱动（SLR 真实引语 + AIHW 人口分布）约束 ChatGPT-4/DeepSeek 生成 20 个 persona，筛出 3 个（Margaret、Zhao、Kamala）并做人工校准；最后对 Apple Health（用药功能）与 Medisafe 做 persona 驱动的认知走查（6 项用药管理任务），暴露出健康驱动、年龄驱动与交叉性（intersectional）失效，并产出可追溯的 inclusivity bug、设计补救与需求更新。核心洞见：facet 之间呈"AND 门"式协同（可用性 = A∧B），年龄与教育/照护等 facet 组合比实际年龄更能预测交互障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字健康软件日益面向身患疾病的终端用户，但开发团队常持有隐含且错误的假设（如"不健康用户技术能力更弱"、愿意接受更长更复杂的流程），导致产品"纸面满足临床目标、实际交互不包容"。既有方法各有缺口：可访问性清单过于粗糙，无法覆盖共病与症状波动；面向具体疾病的指南过窄；通用 persona 只唤起共情却不编码可验证的 facet 端点；GenderMag 等 Magnifier 方法虽有校准过的走查透镜，但未针对健康状态做建模。因此 DH 团队缺少一个紧凑、经验证、以健康为中心、可嵌入日常走查与需求精修的透镜，以便在需求阶段建模健康带来的额外约束，并在评估阶段提前暴露开发者假设偏见。面向对象聚焦 65+ 老年人及慢性病患者：他们在 DH 用户中占比高，且同时受年龄与健康双重约束；面向老年人的 DH 应用也更易获取，因此最适合作为双透镜方法的落地场景。

Method: 方法遵循 InclusiveMag 的四阶段流水线（Mapping → Foundation → Developing → Calibration），分三条并行研究流整合为 Elderly HealthMag。① 左流（HealthMag）：以 7 篇综述类种子文献做前向/后向滚雪球，最终汇集 134 篇（69 篇 CS/SE/DH + 65 篇临床/健康/社科/心理），聚类合并得到 16 个候选 facet；再以 C1–C5 纳入标准（证据性、相关性、取值范围广、非专业者可理解、集合层面可管理）收敛为 7 个候选（Motivation、Received Care、Tech Proficiency、Cognitive Load、Trust & Privacy、co-morbidities、Health Self-efficacy）；对每个 facet 端点给出可观测的交互后果与设计补救。② 右流（Elderly AgeMag）：复用 InclusiveMag（老年邮件用户：technology comfort、attitude toward technology、physical difficulties）与 AgeMag（电商：technical proficiency and attention、risk aversion、vision impairment），合并去重得 6 个 facet，并经专家访谈新增 education level 形成 7 个候选；最终保留 Visual Impairment、Physical Difficulties、Tech Proficiency、Education（并入 Tech Proficiency）、Care received（新增），Risk Aversion 经访谈细化为 Willingness，Attention 因"可缓解"被排除。③ 中流（persona）：把 SLR 中 37 篇论文、逾 20,000 词的真实用户原话需求与 AIHW 人口分布（年龄、性别、语言）作为受约束输入，以 13 个候选 facet + Devi 等人验证的 persona 结构模板提示 ChatGPT-4 与 DeepSeek 生成 20 个 persona，选取覆盖"最健康—中间—最不健康"三端的 3 个（Margaret、Zhao、Kamala），经人机在环校准（真实性、功能性、灵活性、覆盖边界）后修订为柔性 persona。校准方法：对 10 位多学科专家（学术 HCI/SE/数字健康研究者、老年学与临床专家、行业从业者，背景多元）进行半结构化访谈，先让其回忆"年龄包容性 bug"以建立语境，再对候选 facet 做 1–7 强制排序，计算均值与标准差，以定性证据为主、排序统计为辅决定取舍，并新增由 HealthMag 启发的 facet。④ 评估：对 Apple Health（Medications）与 Medisafe 两款广泛使用、含显式包容性设计特性的用药管理应用做认知走查，覆盖 6 项任务（新增药物、记录已服、查看今日日程、改期提醒、共享服药记录、切换显示语言），4 名评估者（3 名用药类 App 开发者 + 1 名作者）按 facet 专门化的问题集逐步记录完成情况（Yes/No/Partial）、1–5 易用度评分、错误/困惑、耗时与建议，非正向结果须截图取证并标注 facet 端点与补救方案；随后 2 小时共识会仲裁并合并 bug 清单，最后各填 SUS（0–100）。

Result: HealthMag 最终 5 个 facet：Motivation（均值 3.3）、Tech Proficiency（3.6）、Received Care（3.7）、Trust & Privacy（4.2）、Health Self-efficacy（4.3）；Cognitive Load 与 co-morbidities 因多被视为特定疾病的后果而非独立 facet 而被排除，转为横切情境变量由其余 facet 与常规可访问性考虑承载。Health Self-efficacy 与 Trust & Privacy 排名方差最大（SD 2.5/2.6）但仍保留，以维持对"信任敏感型"与"需要引导型"画像的区分度。Elderly AgeMag 最终 5 个 facet：Visual Impairment（1.9，SD 0.9，一致性最高）、Physical Difficulties（2.6）、Tech Proficiency（2.7）、Education（1.3，并入 Tech Proficiency）、Care received（新增，源自 HealthMag）；Attention 被排除，Risk Aversion 重构为 Willingness（因"曾被欺骗/受损"经历形成的安全网需求，如可撤销操作与返回路径）。合并去重后形成 8 个 Dual-Mag facet 并分三层：内在能力（"能用"门：Visual Impairment、Physical Difficulties）、健康驱动（"想用"门：Health Motivation、Health Self-efficacy、Trust）、情境使能（"会用"支持：Willingness、Tech Proficiency、Received Care）。专家访谈普遍认可健康与年龄约束的实质性影响，同时指出老年群体内部高度异质，僵化的"老人模式"会掩盖真正的交互变量；facet 间存在相关性（Education 常预测 Tech Proficiency），若假定一面可代偿另一面即产生新的偏见——CW 中一位高学历、高熟练度用户仍未找到 Medisafe 的共享功能，而学历较低者反而成功。走查结果呈稳定梯度：Margaret > Zhao > Kamala，跨两款应用一致。Medisafe SUS 为 76/61/34，Apple Health 为 73/63/44；共享服药记录任务最难（Margaret 在 Medisafe ease=1.0 且未完成），改期任务次之（多为 Partial，ease 2.0–3.5），查看日程与记录已服最易（ease 4.0–5.0）；Kamala 在 Apple Health 上切换语言（僧伽罗语）完全失败（ease 1.5）。评估者认为健康 facet 呈乘性而非加性效应；年龄本身不足以预测设计结果，教育程度与获得的照护同等重要；Medisafe 在功能与用药流程上更聚焦，Apple Health 在数据共享、生态整合与语言可及性等非功能包容特性上更优，说明 UX 质量取决于用户 facet 与应用定位的匹配而非绝对优劣排序。

Conclusion: 本文证明健康与年龄相关的包容性缺口可以通过"系统证据综合 + 专家校准 + persona 驱动认知走查"的组合工作流加以治理，产出 Elderly HealthMag 这一双重透镜 Magnifier 方法：8 个非重叠 facet 以可追溯的 inclusivity bug、设计补救与需求更新形式暴露健康驱动、年龄驱动与交叉性失效，强调以 facet 感知的评估替代单一 SUS 分数比较。作者提出三点方法论贡献：其一，facet-set flexibility——已映射证据的 facet 可作为可复用构件，通过校准重新界定人群范围（本文把"全生命周期年龄"重定为"DH 场景中的老年人"）而非重建方法；其二，dual/multi-lens 组合适用于用户群同时跨越两个多样性维度之时，单一透镜会混淆成因、给出不精确的补救；其三，将 LLM 定位为受约束的合成器而非自主 persona 作者——输入为经过预处理的结构化需求与人口分布、输出须落在已命名 facet 组件内，LLM 生成的 20 个 persona 属中间产物，仍须专家校准覆盖边界、流行度、严重度与交互假设（尤其老年人常为照护者中介使用），以抑制偏见放大与过度泛化。局限包括滚雪球带来的选择与发表偏差、facet 标签的情境敏感性与构念效度、专家判断与评估者先验经验及确认偏差、LLM persona 的刻板化风险、walkthrough 任务覆盖有限，且不主张对任一问题做"仅由年龄/健康导致"的因果归因，结论限于慢性病老年智能手机用药管理场景。作者计划开展真实产品团队的现场部署与纵向研究、与基线方法比较（时间、缺陷产出、修复质量）、扩展到其他 DH 类别与更广人群，并发布证据—facet 映射、提示词与 persona 集以支持复用与复现。

Abstract: Digital health (DH) software is increasingly deployed to populations where many end users live with one or more health conditions. Yet, DH software development teams frequently operate using implicit, incorrect assumptions about these users, resulting in products that under-serve the specific requirements imposed by their age and health conditions. Consequently, while software may meet clinical objectives on paper, it often fails to be inclusive during actual user interaction. To address this, we propose \textbf{\textit{HealthMag}}, a tool inspired by GenderMag designed to help better elicit, model and evaluate requirements for digital health software. We developed HealthMag through systematic mapping and calibration following the InclusiveMag framework. Furthermore, we integrated this with a calibrated version of an existing AgeMag method to create a dual-lens approach: \textbf{\textit{Elderly HealthMag}}, designed to aid requirements, design and evaluation of mHealth software for senior end users. We demonstrate application and utility of Age HealthMag via cognitive walkthroughs in identifying inclusivity biases in current senior user-oriented digital health applications.

</details>


### [195] [From Horizontal Layering to Vertical Integration: A Comparative Study of the AI-Driven Software Development Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22667)
*Chi Zhang,Zehan Li,Ziqian Zhong,Haibing Ma,Dan Xiao,Chen Lin,Ming Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过多案例对比传统企业和AI原生初创公司，发现从水平分层转向垂直集成能实现8-33倍资源消耗降低，归因于超级员工涌现和协调成本消除，提出以人机协作效能为新优化目标，识别AI扭曲效应，提出组织重构策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨生成式AI在软件开发组织中的采纳影响，对比传统企业（棕地）与AI原生初创公司（绿地）的不同组织形态，旨在揭示AI如何重塑软件工程的组织结构和工作流程。

Method: 采用多案例比较研究，对比传统企业（棕地环境）和AI原生初创公司（绿地环境）两种软件开发环境，分析其组织结构和协作模式，运用全要素生产力分析识别AI扭曲效应。

Result: 从水平分层（职能专业化）转向垂直集成（端到端所有权）可将资源消耗降低8-33倍；发现超级员工（经AI增强、跨越传统角色边界的工程师）出现，显著减少跨职能协调成本；识别出AI扭曲效应——降低劳动力规模回报，同时放大技术杠杆效应。

Conclusion: 提出以人机协作效能作为工程组织的主要优化目标，替代个体生产力指标；为管理层提供组织重构策略，包括重新激活资深工程师闲置认知带宽，抑制盲目规模扩张，以实现AI时代的高效软件开发组织设计。

Abstract: This paper examines the organizational implications of Generative AI adoption in software engineering through a multiple-case comparative study. We contrast two development environments: a traditional enterprise (brownfield) and an AI-native startup (greenfield). Our analysis reveals that transitioning from Horizontal Layering (functional specialization) to Vertical Integration (end-to-end ownership) yields 8-fold to 33-fold reductions in resource consumption. We attribute these gains to the emergence of Super Employees, AI-augmented engineers who span traditional role boundaries, and the elimination of inter-functional coordination overhead. Theoretically, we propose Human-AI Collaboration Efficacy as the primary optimization target for engineering organizations, supplanting individual productivity metrics. Our Total Factor Productivity analysis identifies an AI Distortion Effect that diminishes returns to labor scale while amplifying technological leverage. We conclude with managerial strategies for organizational redesign, including the reactivation of idle cognitive bandwidth in senior engineers and the suppression of blind scale expansion.

</details>


### [196] [VarParser: Unleashing the Neglected Power of Variables for LLM-based Log Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22676)
*Jinrui Sun,Tong Jia,Minghua He,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VarParser是一款基于大语言模型的日志解析方法，它从传统关注常量部分的策略转向以变量为中心的策略，通过变量贡献采样、变量缓存和自适应上下文学习等技术，在提高解析准确率的同时显著降低大语言模型调用成本和提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型日志解析器仅关注日志的常量部分，忽略了变量部分的潜在贡献。这种常量中心策略带来了四个关键问题：1）仅依靠常量信息导致的低效日志分组与采样；2）基于常量的缓存需要大量LLM调用，导致解析精度和效率低下；3）提示中包含大量常量词元导致调用成本高；4）结果仅保留占位符，丢失了变量信息带来的系统可见性。

Method: VarParser采用变量中心的日志解析策略，包含三个核心技术：1）变量贡献采样：有效捕获日志变量部分并利用其对解析的贡献；2）变量中心解析缓存：减少LLM调用次数；3）自适应变量感知上下文学习：优化学习过程。此外，通过引入变量单元，保留丰富的变量信息，增强日志解析结果的完整性。

Result: 在大规模数据集上的大量实验表明，VarParser相比现有方法实现了更高的准确率，同时显著提高了解析效率并降低了LLM调用成本。

Conclusion: 变量中心策略能够有效解决传统常量中心日志解析方法的局限性，通过充分挖掘和利用日志变量信息，VarParser在精度、效率和成本方面均表现出显著优势，为下游任务（如异常检测和故障诊断）提供了更完整的日志解析结果。

Abstract: Logs serve as a primary source of information for engineers to diagnose failures in large-scale online service systems. Log parsing, which extracts structured events from massive unstructured log data, is a critical first step for downstream tasks like anomaly detection and failure diagnosis. With advances in large language models (LLMs), leveraging their strong text understanding capabilities has proven effective for accurate log parsing. However, existing LLM-based log parsers all focus on the constant part of logs, ignoring the potential contribution of the variable part to log parsing. This constant-centric strategy brings four key problems. First, inefficient log grouping and sampling with only constant information. Second, a relatively large number of LLM invocations due to constant-based cache, leading to low log parsing accuracy and efficiency. Third, a relatively large number of consumed constant tokens in prompts leads to high LLM invocation costs. At last, these methods only retain placeholders in the results, losing the system visibility brought by variable information in logs.
  Facing these problems, we propose a variable-centric log parsing strategy named VarParser. Through variable contribution sampling, variable-centric parsing cache, and adaptive variable-aware in-context learning, our approach can efficiently capture the variable parts of logs and leverage their contributions to parsing. By introducing variable units, we preserve rich variable information, enhancing the integrity of log parsing results. Extensive evaluations on large-scale datasets demonstrate that VarParser achieves higher accuracy compared to existing methods, significantly improving parsing efficiency while reducing the LLM invocation costs.

</details>


### [197] [AutoMerge: Search-Based Model Merging Framework for Effective Model Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22748)
*You Lu,Jiyang Zhang,Bihuan Chen,Chaofeng Sha,Dingji Wang,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了LLM以外的五个模型合并技术在不同架构模型上的表现，发现现有方法在不同领域效果不一致，且对超参数敏感，进而提出了AutoMerge框架以提高模型合并的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型复用是深度学习中的重要范式，模型合并（model merging）作为无需训练的方法在LLM领域取得了成功。但现有研究尚未系统探究该方法在其他架构和领域中的适用性，存在研究空白。

Method: 本研究评估了五种模型合并技术在三个领域（LLM、图像分类、自动驾驶）和三种不同模型架构上的表现，并基于观察提出了AutoMerge框架：该框架将复杂模型分割为异质模块，并通过系统搜索来确定各模块的最佳合并技术及超参数配置。

Result: 实证结果表明，直接将现有模型合并技术应用于LLM以外的模型架构和领域时，效果高度不一致且明显差于LLM；单一技术难以处理模型内部的异质结构特性，且效果对超参数配置高度敏感，限制了其广泛应用。

Conclusion: 模型合并技术的有效性受模型架构、领域和超参数配置的影响显著，AutoMerge通过块级合并和搜索策略能够有效应对异质性，为跨架构、跨领域的模型复用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Software reuse has long been recognized as a critical and widely studied topic in software engineering, offering substantial benefits in reducing development costs, improving software quality, and enhancing operational efficiency. This paradigm extends into deep learning through model reuse. Recently, model merging has emerged in the domain of large language models (LLMs) as a training-free approach that takes multiple task-specific models with the same architecture as source models and merges them without retraining, enhancing model reuse within LLMs. However, no prior work has systematically investigated whether such an approach can be effectively applied to other deep learning models with different architectures across domains. To bridge this gap, we present the first systematic study that evaluates five model merging techniques on three distinct model architectures across three domains: LLMs, image classification, and autonomous driving. Our findings reveal that directly applying existing model merging techniques leads to highly inconsistent results and falls notably short of their success within LLMs. Moreover, a single model merging technique often fails to handle the heterogeneous structural properties within a model, limiting its applicability to different model architectures across domains. Furthermore, the effectiveness of model merging techniques is highly sensitive to hyperparameter configurations, thereby constraining their potential for broader adoption. Inspired by these insights, we propose AutoMerge, a novel search-based model merging framework that first segments complex models into multiple heterogeneous blocks and then systematically explores the merging space to identify the merging technique and its hyperparameter configuration.

</details>


### [198] [Constructing Safety Cases for AI Systems: A Reusable Template Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22773)
*Sung Une Lee,Liming Zhu,Md Shamsujjoha,Liming Dong,Qinghua Lu,Jieshan Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向AI系统的可复用安全论证（Safety Case）模板框架：以"主张—论证—证据"（CAE）为主干，建立AI专用分类体系（3类主张、6类论证、7类证据族）与显式推理逻辑映射（演绎/归纳/溯因/统计/类比），配套4个端到端安全论证模式（发现驱动评估、无真值的边际风险、持续演化、阈值比较），并嵌入Builder—Validator—Registry动态保障流水线；以政府AI招投标评分系统为案例验证边际风险模式（人—人不一致率3.0% vs AI—人2.8%，ΔR=−0.2%，阈值δ=5%，α=0.95）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全论证（航空、核电）依赖明确系统边界、稳定架构与已知失效模式，其论证逻辑是演绎与层级的，依赖"设计充分性"和"证据完备性"。现代生成式/智能体式AI系统恰恰相反：能力由低层训练目标涌现而非逐行设计；行为随提示词变化；风险随微调、脚手架与部署语境漂移；评测常无稳定真值（只能相对于人类或既有自动化基线作边际推断）；底层模型由第三方维护、更新不可控，使"认证后冻结配置"假设失效；监管广泛使用计算量/能力/安全分等量化阈值但阈值彼此重叠且情境依赖；能力涌现与规格解耦；模型行为与上下文工程紧耦合。此外，既有AI安全论证工作（frontier AI安全论证、cyber inability模板、DSCMS动态管理、控制安全论证草图等）彼此割裂：缺乏统一的CAE分类、术语不一致、关注孤立要素而未打通主张—论证—证据全链，且很少刻画"通过评测/红队/对抗探测发现新能力与失效模式"这一迭代过程，也很少反映部署后保障与证据可追溯性。对112篇文献的综合分析进一步暴露三个普遍缺陷：可接受性判据欠规范（使用"不劣于基线"却未定义基线的保证状态与量化阈值/失败预算）；证据质量准则与可追溯性声明不一致（未规定独立性、覆盖率、时效性、可复现性、链接关系）；部署后保障碎片化（仅≤10%研究将主张与运营监控、治理动作挂钩）。

Method: 方法学部分采用Kitchenham准则的系统性文献综述（SLR）：以PICOC界定范围（对象为AI安全论证/CAE/GSN文献；干预为构造、分析或操作化安全论证的方法、流程、模板、工具与分类体系；成果为CAE分类与模式库；排除无安全论证关联的一般AI伦理治理文献），形成RQ1（适合AI安全论证的主张/论证/证据分类体系）与RQ2（可复用模板与模式及其案例应用）。检索为四段式：检索式（三个概念簇以AND/OR布尔组合，含截断与同义词）在四个数字库检索得1235篇；以9篇种子论文滚雪球得807篇；去重并按排除标准剔除1588篇、按纳入标准剔除113篇；再对入选文献做Google Scholar后向滚雪球得1070篇并新增46篇；最终112篇原始研究进入提取与综合，质量评估采用11项1–5分量表（均分<2.0剔除11篇；最终均分3.37，范围2.73–4.55，86%≥3.0），并由至少两位作者独立交叉核对。综合方法为混合方法：描述性统计 + 主题综合（并显式区分"核心引用"与"推断映射"以说明解释偏差风险）。框架构建部分：定义AI安全论证的关键概念（安全论证、CAE、分类体系、模板、模式）；给出AI安全生态流水线（Builder/plugins、Validator、Registry，分别承担构造、验证、存档与版本治理）；剖析传统安全论证基础（FTA/FMEA、冗余与程序性屏障）被AI破坏的六项机制（部署后能力发现、无稳定真值评测、系统所有者控制外的模型更新、阈值式可接受性、能力涌现与规格解耦、模型行为与上下文工程紧耦合）；构建CAE分类体系——主张：断言式（绝对/边际）、约束式（普通约束/包络约束）、能力受限式（控制型：执行/访问/工具使用/模态受限；内禀型：知识受限/目标受限）；论证：演示型（分层安全/屏障）、比较型（比较论证/类比论证）、风险型（阈值型/准则型）、因果解释型（可依赖性/根因）、能力导向型（控制论证/内禀论证）、规范符合型（准则型/可依赖性），并给出与演绎、归纳、溯因、统计、类比五种推理逻辑的对齐表（Table 7）以及"整合推理与元保障"（每种逻辑配有效条件/通过条件）；证据：经验型、比较型基准、模型化风险分析、专家推导、形式化验证、运营与现场数据、机制/可解释性七族，各自标注质量关注点与独立性/覆盖率/时效性/可复现性/代表性准则。模板被设计为可组合：完整安全论证由多条CAE链拼接，可独立替换具体主张实例与证据产物。四个端到端模式各按"问题/适用主张/论证功能与推理逻辑/证据族与质量条件/示例结构"五段式展开：①发现驱动评估模式；②无真值边际风险模式（以可预测性/一致性、能力类、游戏类三类代理指标三角互证，以Δ≤δ、置信≥α表达非劣性）；③持续演化模式（变更控制、版本间非劣、滚动SPI、治理审计轨迹）；④阈值比较模式（阈值分解、预声明聚合函数F(T1−Tn)、阈值优先级的规范论证）。案例部分为真实政府场景：AI招投标评估系统，因无绝对真值故选用边际风险模式；主张为"AI+人工流程在相关绩效与诚信指标上至少不劣于原人+人流程"；选定比较型（归纳，主论证）与风险型/阈值型（统计，补充）论证，规范型未选用；定义多维风险向量R（绩效、可靠性、安全、安全性、公平、隐私、合规、成本、韧性）与边际风险MR=ΔR=R_AI+Human−R_Human+Human；证据来自200例合成招投标案例的双盲对照评测（同一协议下测量评分者间一致性、公平性偏差、理由质量），经自助法bootstrap显著性检验与置信区间估计。

Result: 产出一套系统性、可组合、可复用的AI安全论证基础：①整合式CAE分类体系（3类主张/6类论证/7类证据族，且均为正交透镜而非互斥分类，允许跨类重叠），每类均绑定明确的推理逻辑、证据要求与质量准则；②模板元模型（主张模板含主/子主张与假设、论证模板含多种分解与推理结构、证据模板含类型与质量标准），并规范每个模板实例必须显式声明比较器/阈值、证据质量准则与触发重新验证的更新触发条件；③四个可实例化模式库，覆盖发现驱动评估、无真值边际风险、持续演化、阈值可接受性决策；④嵌入连续评测流水线的动态保障机制（主张—实时指标—治理产物链接，SPI与"可核查安全论证"式持续刷新）；⑤对112篇文献的定量与主题综合，确认当前实践以"条件性主张+经验证据"为主流形态，并量化确认三项缺陷。案例结论：AI—人配置评审不一致率2.8%低于人—人基线3.0%，MR=−0.2%，在δ=5%、α=0.95下满足非劣判据，说明无绝对真值场景下可构建可辩护的相对安全论证，并建议持续监控一致性与公平性指标以维持阈值内运行，AI评审可在保持一致性/公平/透明前提下部分替代人工评审以降低工作量。

Conclusion: 该研究将安全论证从"每个项目手工定制的文档"升级为面向AI的工程化资产：分类体系提供统一术语与可审计的论证结构，模板提供标准化半结构化论证骨架，模式库提供跨系统可复用的端到端推理片段，并明确以演绎（设计与控制）、归纳/统计（评测与非劣性）、溯因（异常解释与缓解）、类比（既有保障迁移）的多元推理组合来适配AI"持续认知不完备"的不确定性特征，从而使保障从静态认证转为持续学习过程。作者明确声明这不是完备方案：动态安全论证的可扩展性（证据过载、自动重写论证结构、缺乏统一标准与可信数据管道）、跨监管制度的互操作性（EU AI Act等法哲学差异、法律要求到可度量技术主张的映射）、评测可复现性（标准化测试协议、共享基准与事故库、开放验证协议）仍是关键挑战；并且不建模"验证过程"本身，仅把验证产物视为证据输入，与"可验证性优先"的AI工程路线互补。后续工作方向：把模板操作化为工具支持的安全论证模式库、形式化跨推理模式的不确定性传播、开发动态系统的复合安全指标、标准化基于比较器的边际风险评测、用LLM自动起草安全论证要素的人机混合范式（LLM作起草加速器、人作验证）、将护栏日志/AgentOps可观测性数据并入证据仓库、以及建立法律要求到CAE可度量主张的系统映射方法。

Abstract: Safety cases, structured arguments that a system is acceptably safe, are becoming central to the governance of AI systems. Yet, traditional safety-case practices from aviation or nuclear engineering rely on well-specified system boundaries, stable architectures, and known failure modes. Modern AI systems such as generative and agentic AI are the opposite. Their capabilities emerge unpredictably from low-level training objectives, their behaviour varies with prompts, and their risk profiles shift through fine-tuning, scaffolding, or deployment context. This study examines how safety cases are currently constructed for AI systems and why classical approaches fail to capture these dynamics. It then proposes a framework of reusable safety-case templates, each following a predefined structure of claims, arguments, and evidence tailored for AI systems. The framework introduces comprehensive taxonomies for AI-specific claim types (assertion-based, constrained-based, capability-based), argument types (demonstrative, comparative, causal/explanatory, risk-based, and normative), and evidence families (empirical, mechanistic, comparative, expert-driven, formal methods, operational/field data, and model-based). Each template is illustrated through end-to-end patterns addressing distinctive challenges such as evaluation without ground truth, dynamic model updates, and threshold-based risk decisions. The result is a systematic, composable, and reusable approach to constructing and maintaining safety cases that are credible, auditable, and adaptive to the evolving behaviour of generative and frontier AI systems.

</details>


### [199] [Understanding on the Edge: LLM-generated Boundary Test Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22791)
*Sabinakhon Akbarova,Felix Dobslaw,Robert Feldt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是首项关于「LLM 生成边界测试解释」的探索性实证研究：通过问卷（27名软件专业人士对20个边界对的GPT-4.1解释在清晰性、正确性、完整性、有用性四个维度上按五点Likert评分）与6人半结构化访谈，评估LLM能否向测试者解释为何某组输入-输出对构成行为边界。结果总体正面（63.5%评分为4-5，17%为1-2，均值3.7-3.8），但存在幻觉（如把199→200误说成年份位数从3位变4位）、术语未定义、缺少权威引用等缺陷；研究据此提出面向LLM边界解释工具的7项需求清单（自适应详略、权威引用、显式结构、多样例证、上下文假设说明、交互式对话、工具链集成），并以改进提示词做了初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界值分析/测试（BVT）虽被广泛使用，却长期缺乏精确形式化定义，其实际适用性高度依赖人工解释；在输入空间大、数据结构复杂、规格不完整的系统中，等价类划分与边界识别极难自动化。近年Feldt与Dobslaw等提出「boundariness（边界度）」度量并开发了SETBVE等可自动发现边界候选的搜索式工具，但识别只是第一步——边界往往缺乏明显正确性线索，测试者必须理解「为何该输入-输出对位于边界上」，否则自动化发现无法被信任、无法落地。此外已有研究表明，缺少可读理由的自动生成测试用例往往被开发者丢弃（可读性价值），AI作者身份一旦揭示也可能削弱信任。同时LLM在测试生成、代码审查与自主测试代理上已取得进展，但其「解释」能力在BVT中从未被实证评估。因此本文以解释作为研究计划的低风险入口，回答两个问题：LLM能否向测试者解释软件边界（清晰性、正确性、完整性、有用性），以及什么样的边界解释才是好的、如何被使用。

Method: 采用顺序混合方法（sequential mixed-methods）。①对象与材料：选择4个黑盒被测函数（Email合法性校验、Bytecount字节数SI格式化、Date公历日期构造、BMI计算与分级），各以伪代码签名+一句话描述呈现（不暴露实现语言与源码）。每个FUT取5个边界对，共20个；Email由ChatGPT辅助提示生成，其余取自SETBVE开源数据集，先按程序导数（boundariness）选Top-100，再以贪心max-min策略取10个多样性最高的边界对，按Dobslaw等的Valid/Erroneous分类（VV/VE/EE）取5个并优先短输入，三位作者共同复核语义有效性与多样性。②解释生成：2025年5月经OpenAI API用GPT-4.1（temperature=0.2，依据Oli等低温度更正确简洁的结论），系统提示设定为资深测试员并规定3项核心要求与5条风格准则，解释限150字符以降低认知负荷；通过比较GPT-4.0与4.1输出的余弦相似度（0.84±0.09）论证模型选择。③参与者：邮件与LinkedIn便利抽样，27份问卷（13研究者/14从业者，中位经验10年；AI/ML 18、企业后端14、Web/云9、嵌入式6、移动5、游戏3；BVT熟悉度：5不熟悉、12一般、8较熟悉、2专家），6人（3+3）参加30分钟半结构化访谈。④流程控制：问卷阶段不透露解释由LLM生成（避免AI框架引发的负面效应），访谈阶段才揭示；问卷在Netigate上进行，平均用时65分钟，每个边界对同时展示输入输出、解释与四维度Likert矩阵（含tooltip定义），各项必答，另设自由文本与最终工具使用意愿问题；访谈提纲由问卷反馈迭代形成，经同意录音并自动转写+人工校对。⑤分析：问卷做描述性统计（均值、中位数、标准差）与按FUT/维度聚合；自由文本与访谈采用Braun & Clarke反思性主题分析，一人初编码、全作者协同复核。⑥效度威胁控制：先做预实验确认边界对本身可理解、始终配对展示、匿名问卷以减轻社会赞许偏差、提供完整复现包（提示词、边界对、匿名原始数据、脚本）。
