Abstract: The key to building trustworthy Large Language Models (LLMs) lies in endowing them with inherent uncertainty expression capabilities to mitigate the hallucinations that restrict their high-stakes applications. However, existing RL paradigms such as GRPO often suffer from Advantage Bias due to binary decision spaces and static uncertainty rewards, inducing either excessive conservatism or overconfidence. To tackle this challenge, this paper unveils the root causes of reward hacking and overconfidence in current RL paradigms incorporating uncertainty-based rewards, based on which we propose the UnCertainty-Aware Policy Optimization (UCPO) framework. UCPO employs Ternary Advantage Decoupling to separate and independently normalize deterministic and uncertain rollouts, thereby eliminating advantage bias. Furthermore, a Dynamic Uncertainty Reward Adjustment mechanism is introduced to calibrate uncertainty weights in real-time according to model evolution and instance difficulty. Experimental results in mathematical reasoning and general tasks demonstrate that UCPO effectively resolves the reward imbalance, significantly improving the reliability and calibration of the model beyond their knowledge boundaries.

</details>


### [246] [Real-Time Aligned Reward Model beyond Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22664)
*Zixuan Huang,Xin Xia,Yuxi Ren,Jianbin Zheng,Xuefeng Xiao,Hongyan Xie,Li Huaqiu,Songshi Liang,Zhongxiang Dai,Fuzhen Zhuang,Jianxin Li,Yikun Ban,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出轻量级 RLHF 框架 R2M（Real-Time Aligned Reward Model）：把策略模型（policy）末层隐状态作为"策略反馈"注入奖励模型打分头，并用时间步余弦权重调度融合原始 Reward Token Embedding，配合每轮 RL 后的迭代式轻量 RM 优化（GREBT 损失：Bradley-Terry + Group Reward Entropy）。在 UltraFeedback→AlpacaEval 2/MT-Bench 与 TL;DR 摘要任务上，R2M 相对 RLOO 提升长度控制胜率 2.9%–6.1%、原始胜率 5.2%–8.0%、TL;DR 胜率 6.3%，RM 在 UltraFeedback 测试集准确率由 72.3% 提升至 77.4%/78.6%，而额外算力开销几乎可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF 的核心痛点是奖励过优化（reward overoptimization）：策略会去利用 RM 学到的伪模式（长度、markdown、n-gram、道歉语等）刷高奖励，而不再忠实对齐人类意图。根源在于 RM 只在有限偏好数据上训练、且在 RL 过程中保持冻结，而策略分布持续漂移（distribution shift），二者错位（misalignment）不断扩大，导致奖励偏差累积。已有缓解方案要么依赖表层语义/不确定性估计（Coste et al. 2023；Eisenstein et al. 2023；Zhang et al. 2024a），要么需要昂贵的 RM 全量重训练（Lang et al. 2024；Liu et al. 2024），在效率与鲁棒性之间难以兼顾。作者由此提出核心问题：能否在不改动 RLHF 算法主体、不引入额外标注数据的前提下，实现 RM 对策略模型的"实时对齐"？

Method: 1）动机验证（Sec.3）：在 LLaMA3-8B 上提取第 6/12/18/24/30 层隐状态，证明同偏好样本对的隐状态相似度显著高于异偏好对且随层深增大差距扩大；且隐状态相似度与 RM 给出的奖励绝对差呈强负相关。Theorem 3.1 给出理论上界：融合后对齐质量 γ^(t)>0 时 ε_R2M ≤ √(1−γ^(t))·C + ΔD^(t)·L 严格小于 vanilla RM 的 C + ΔD^(t)·L。
2）结构（Sec.4.1）：在 RM 中新增"策略反馈"数据流——采样阶段取策略末层隐状态 h∈R^{S×Dp}，在奖励标注阶段用 Sequence-to-Token Cross Attention（RM 的 RTE 作 query，策略隐状态作 key/value）得到聚合 RTE；再用基于训练步的余弦调度权重 ω(t)=max(½cos(t/T·π)+½, Ω) 将原始 RTE 与聚合 RTE 加权得到最终 RTE，经打分头 φ 输出标量奖励。
3）迭代优化（Sec.4.2）：策略前向时在最后一个 epoch 刷新隐状态（零额外开销）；每个 RL 步后新增 RM 优化阶段，用组内最高/最低分构造偏好对，损失 L_GREBT=(1−α)L_BT+αL_GRE，其中 GRE 为组内奖励分布的负熵（标准化后 softmax），以缓解组内退化（group degeneration）。仅更新 cross-attention 与打分头，冻结 RM 的 LLM 主体（参数量开销极小）。Theorem 4.1 证明 GRE 项严格降低组退化度且改善幅度随 α 单调递增。
4）多节点扩展：把轻量 cross-attention 副本放在策略侧，通信量从 B×S×Dp 降到 2×B×Dp，且不随序列长度增长；支持异步更新。

Result: 对话任务（Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct / LLaMA3-8B-Instruct，策略 UltraFeedback，RM 为 Skywork-Reward-V2-Llama-3.1-8B）：RLOO+R2M 在 AlpacaEval 2 上 LC 24.8%/34.5%、WR 31.2%/38.2%，MT-Bench 6.7%/7.3%，均优于 vanilla RLOO、GRPO、ReMax、REINFORCE++ 及 Pretrained RM、Iterative RM_Head 等基线，且输出长度更短（2011 vs 2186）显示较少长度 hack；GRPO+R2M 同样取得最佳/次佳。摘要任务（Pythia-2.8B-TL;DR）：WR 81.0%/81.6%，显著高于 GRPO 75.2%、RLOO 75.3%。RM 能力：UltraFeedback 测试集准确率 72.3%→77.4%（Qwen）/78.6%（LLaMA3）。消融显示 R2M w/ Noise、w/o Train 均大幅退化（注入未训练的反馈≈注入噪声），去掉 BT 损失 LC/WR 分别降 3.0/2.5，去掉 GRE 分别降 2.2/2.0。分析显示 R2M 在保持奖励趋势一致的同时系统性给出更高奖励、鼓励更激进而有效的策略更新且未触发奖励过优化；计算开销相比全量 RM 更新大幅下降，相对 vanilla RLOO 几乎可忽略。

Conclusion: R2M 证明"超越语义的策略内部状态"可作为实时对齐信号：无需额外标注数据、无需改动 RLHF 核心机制，通过轻量级迭代 RM 更新即可同时缓解奖励过优化、提升 RM 准确性与最终策略质量，并保持极低训练开销。其贡献在于开辟了"利用策略模型实时反馈改进奖励模型"这一新方向，同时给出通信量可控的多节点部署方案与关于组退化、GRE 项无人工抬分/抗噪鲁棒性的理论分析。局限在于依赖策略与 RM 的前向可得性、实验集中于 3B–8B 规模与对话/摘要任务，且隐状态相似度与偏好/奖励的相关性仅是相关性证据而非因果解释。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is a pivotal technique for aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences, yet it is susceptible to reward overoptimization, in which policy models overfit to the reward model, exploit spurious reward patterns instead of faithfully capturing human intent. Prior mitigations primarily relies on surface semantic information and fails to efficiently address the misalignment between the reward model (RM) and the policy model caused by continuous policy distribution shifts. This inevitably leads to an increasing reward discrepancy, exacerbating reward overoptimization. To address these limitations, we introduce R2M (Real-Time Aligned Reward Model), a novel lightweight RLHF framework. R2M goes beyond vanilla reward models that solely depend on the semantic representations of a pretrained LLM. Instead, it leverages the evolving hidden states of the policy (namely policy feedback) to align with the real-time distribution shift of the policy during the RL process. This work points to a promising new direction for improving the performance of reward models through real-time utilization of feedback from policy models.

</details>


### [247] [Best-of-Q: Improving VLM agents with Q-function Action Ranking at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22701)
*Emilien Biré,María Santos,Kai Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Best-of-Q 范式：冻结 VLM 策略，仅在推理阶段由离线训练的轻量级 Q 函数（~11M 参数）对 VLM 提出的 N=3 个候选动作做重排序，取代策略微调。在 WebVoyager（590 任务）上，Qwen2.5-VL-7B 成功率从 38.8% 提升至 55.7%，GPT-4.1 从 82.4% 提升至 88.8%，Qwen2.5-VL-72B 从 69.1% 提升至 76.6%；训练仅用约 30 万转移，成本-性能优于 prompting、随机选择与 VLM-as-selector 基线，且效果接近全量微调的 Holo1-7B（59.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web 与操作系统等快速变化的数字环境中，VLM 作为智能体策略存在适应性问题，通常需通过在线 RL 或大规模模仿学习微调数十亿参数模型才能提升，成本高昂、数据采集困难且迭代周期长。作者希望在不重训策略、不进行昂贵在线交互的前提下，于推理阶段直接改进 VLM 智能体的决策质量。其核心洞察是：VLM 已具备较强的语义理解与动作生成能力，但常因"贪心单步选择"失误；将"高容量动作生成"与"基于价值的动作选择"解耦即可低成本地纠正这些次优选择。

Method: 问题建模为稀疏奖励 MDP（状态含截图、任务指令、动作历史，奖励为任务成功 1/失败 0）。架构解耦为两部分：冻结的 VLM 策略负责生成 N 个带独立 reasoning 的多样化候选动作（系统提示强制多动作类型与结构化输出）；轻量 Q 函数（Qwen2.5-VL-3B 冻结作特征提取器，分别池化得到 state/action/task 三路嵌入，拼接后经投影与 MLP [1024,1024,512,256,128,64] 输出标量 Q 值，约 11M 可训练参数）为动作打分排序，执行 argmax Q 的动作。Q 函数用 IQL（Kostrikov et al. 2021）离线训练：expectile 回归学 V(s)（GPT-4.1 数据 τ=0.8，Qwen 数据 τ=0.7），再以 r+γV(s') 为目标更新 Q，规避 OOD 查询与分布漂移。数据采集采用迭代自改进循环：ε-greedy（ε=0.5）多候选探索收集种子数据 → 训练初始 Q → 用 Best-of-Q 贪心探索式收集高质量轨迹 → 并入训练集重训（5 次初始采集 + 4 轮迭代，最终约 30 万转移）。推理时额外调用 Holo1.5-7B 定位器获取点击/输入的精确坐标。

Result: 主结果（WebVoyager 590 patched 任务，3 次运行平均）：GPT-4.1 82.4→88.8%，Qwen2.5-VL-7B 38.8→55.7%（+16.9），Qwen2.5-VL-72B 69.1→76.6%；成功所需平均步数亦下降（72B 9.4→8.8，7B 10.9→10.3）。随机动作基线显著退化（GPT-4.1 降至 72.1%），证明增益来自有依据的选择机制而非候选数量本身。以 VLM 直接做动作选择器（7B/72B/GPT-4.1）均低于 Best-of-Q 且成本更高（68.2%/73.5%/73.6% 等）。与 Holo1-7B（59.2%，1.5M 专家轨迹微调）相比，无微调的 Best-of-Q 达 55.7%，显示更优成本-性能权衡。消融：推理 N 与训练 N 匹配最佳（N=3 训练在 N=3 时 55.7%，N=5 训练在 N=5 时 53.4%），增大 N 会引入分布漂移与"Refresh/Restart"类占位动作；更换更大的嵌入器（7B、Holo1-7B）无明显提升（55.0%/55.3%）。样本效率：7B 在数条轨迹后即超基线，约 27 轮后进入平台期。失败分析（Google Flights 任务，以 GPT-4.1 轨迹为"golden action"）：50.3% 步骤中基座 VLM 未提出正确动作（硬上限），正确动作存在时 Q 仅选中 13.6%、失败 36.2%。可靠性分析：Best-of-Q 以 pass@1 达到 pass@2 的平均水平但任务级方差显著更低，去随机化了基座的随机性。泛化：在 3 个新航班域（Skyscanner/Momondo/Cruisecritic，120 任务）上训练的 Q 函数迁移到 Google-Flights 达 42.7%（vs 同域 48.3%，prompting 26.2%）；跨策略动作空间迁移亦有效（GPT-4.1 数据训练的 Q 指导 72B 达 69.5%）。

Conclusion: Best-of-Q 证明了一种无需策略重训、可直接用于推理期的 VLM 智能体增强范式：把动作生成与价值驱动选择解耦，用离线训练的轻量 Q 函数重排候选动作，可在多个 VLM 骨干（含不可微调的闭源模型）上稳定提升成功率与效率，且成本远低于微调或 VLM-as-selector。核心局限是性能上限由基座 VLM 的动作提议召回率决定（半数步骤未提出正确动作），未来应结合提升候选多样性与质量的提议机制；同时 Q 函数本身的判断仍不完美，存在价值歧义与数据覆盖不足导致的误选。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have become powerful backbones for agents to autonomously operate in digital environments like the web and operating systems. However, these models suffer from inadaptability to fast-changing environments like the web, which can be alleviated by fine-tuning requiring expansive model training and data collection. In this work, we introduce a novel paradigm for enhancing agentic VLM policies at inference without policy retraining. Fundamentally, our approach decouples the VLM's role as a high-capacity action proposer from the final action selection mechanism. We keep the VLM policy frozen and use it to generate a set of candidate actions for a given state. Then, a lightweight, offline-trained Q-function reranks these candidates, and the agent executes the action with the highest estimated value. The main contribution is to apply the Q-function directly during inference for immediate policy improvement, and not offline to relabel data for policy retraining. We demonstrate on the academic WebVoyager benchmark that our method significantly boosts agent success rates, improving a Qwen2.5-VL-7B agent from 38.8% to 55.7% and a proprietary GPT-4.1 agent from 82.4% to 88.8%.

</details>


### [248] [A Step Back: Prefix Importance Ratio Stabilizes Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22718)
*Shiye Lei,Zhihao Cheng,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，现有大模型 RL 后训练方法普遍使用的 token 级重要性采样比只是对"前缀重要性比"的粗糙近似，在大 off-policy 偏移（策略滞后）下会导致训练不稳定甚至崩溃。作者从策略梯度定理重新推导，证明理论上严格的修正是累积前缀重要性比 ρ_{1:t}=∏ρ_i；为解决其数值爆炸与长度偏置问题，提出 MinPRO：用前缀中最小 token 比 ρ̄_t=min_{i<t}ρ_i 与当前 token 比 ρ_t 相乘作为非累积代理项（软截断）。在 Qwen3-8B/14B/30B-A3B (MoE) 的 DAPO-Math-17K 数学推理后训练上，MinPRO 在 n=2（32–47 次参数更新滞后）时平均 pass@1 达 59.4/62.6，优于 GRPO/GSPO/CISPO/M2PO 等强基线，且在 n=0、4、8、16 及 pass@k、MoE 规模下均保持最优或最优并列，训练奖励曲线更平稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的 RL 后训练（尤其是数学推理）通常采用 off-policy 工作流：用较旧的行为策略 π_old 批量生成 rollout，再切分成多个 mini-batch 更新当前策略 π；异步 RL 框架进一步引入策略滞后（policy lag），这能显著提升吞吐并减少因响应长度不均衡造成的 GPU 空闲，但由此产生的 rollout 分布偏移在高度 off-policy 时会导致优化不稳定甚至训练崩溃（奖励骤降、熵爆炸）。为纠正分布偏移，现有方法（GRPO、GSPO、CISPO、M2PO 等）几乎都采用 token 级重要性采样比 ρ_t=π_θ/π_old，主要因为其计算简单、数值稳定。但作者指出：从 off-policy 策略梯度严格推导，正确的修正项应是前缀（序列前缀）重要性比 ρ_{1:t}，token 级加权并未显式校正前缀分布偏移；且 ρ_{1:t} 随序列长度呈乘性增长，而 LLM rollout 常超过 10,000 token，因此 off-policyness 越大，token 级近似与真实前缀比的偏离越剧烈，从而引发不稳定。已有的稳定化工作（BAPO/M2PO 改截断、AsymRE 调奖励基线）多为启发式，缺乏理论依据。

Method: 【理论推导】以自回归 LLM 为设定，用 REINFORCE/log-derivative trick 证明策略梯度定理（Lemma 1），通过 score-function 基线不变性（Lemma 2）消去 R_<t 与 V^π，得到自包含形式的策略梯度；再对前缀边缘分布 o_{1:t} 施加 change-of-measure 恒等式（P_θ/P_old 的比值），利用自回归分解 P_θ(o_{1:t})=∏π_θ(o_i|o_<i)，得 Theorem 1：off-policy 梯度严格为 ∑_t E_{o_{1:t}~π_old}[ρ_{1:t} ∇log π_θ(o_t|o_<t) A^π]，其中 ρ_{1:t}=∏_{i≤t}ρ_i 为前缀重要性比。直接使用它存在两大问题：累积乘积易产生极端值/大方差，且极端值集中在序列末尾，引入强长度偏置。【MinPRO 目标】以非累积代理替换累积量：ρ̄_t = min_{i<t} ρ_i，ρ_{1:t} ≈ ρ̄_t · ρ_t；目标函数沿用 CISPO 的软截断形式——sg(clip(ρ̄_t ρ_t, 1−ε_low, 1+ε_high)) · Â_t · log π_θ(o_t|q,o_<t)，损失按 token-mean 聚合，ε_low=1、ε_high=4。直觉上，当前缀累计比极小时说明该轨迹在当前策略下已近乎不可能，此时即使 ρ_t 正常也应抑制该 token 的梯度更新。实现上在 VeRL 的 core_algos.py 中以 "minpro" 注册：log_ratio clamp 到 (−20,20) 后 exp 得 ratio，用 masked_fill(inf)+cummin 求前缀最小值（非包含当前 token），clip 后除以 ratio.detach() 构造有效权重，并复用 M2PO 的 stale-rollout FIFO 队列机制。【实验设置】DAPO-Math-17K，Qwen3-8B-Base / 14B-Base（FSDP）与 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base（Megatron，MoE），vLLM 推理，max response 20480 token，关闭 KL，critic-free，G=8，batch/mini-batch 512/32（14B 为 256/16），每 global step 16 次参数更新；通过 rollout buffer 延迟 n 个 global step 控制策略滞后（n=2 时滞后 32–47 次更新）。评测 AMC23、AIME24、AIME25、MATH500、Olympiad、Minerva、GSM8K 的 pass@1/pass@k。对比 GRPO、GSPO、CISPO、M2PO、AsymRE、VESPO、SNIB。

Result: 1) 失稳诊断：Qwen3-8B-Base 上，on/off-policy 对比显示硬截断类（GRPO、GSPO）在 off-policy 下奖励显著更低且剧烈振荡、熵动力学不稳；软截断的 CISPO 更糟，on-policy 与 off-policy 均出现奖励骤降、熵爆炸的训练崩溃——说明 token 级修正在大滞后下失效。2) 稳定性：n=2 下 8B/14B 训练奖励曲线中，GRPO/GSPO/CISPO 剧烈波动，而 MinPRO 全程奖励更高且曲线平稳，甚至优于专门面向大 off-policy 的 M2PO；MoE 30B-A3B 上同样保持最高最稳奖励曲线与更稳的 pass@1 轨迹。3) 峰值性能：8B 平均 pass@1 为 59.4（GRPO 46.4、GSPO 50.0、CISPO 56.8、M2PO 58.7）；14B 为 62.6（M2PO 62.1、CISPO 61.7、GSPO 60.4、GRPO 51.6），至少领先 0.5 分。4) 鲁棒性：n=4 时 58.3，比 M2PO 高 1.4 分；n=0（近 on-policy）仍最佳 59.3；n=8/n=16 分别为 57.3/57.2，仅比 n=4 低约 1 分，体现出对极大策略滞后（128–271 次更新）的不敏感。5) pass@k：8B/14B 在 AMC23/AIME24/AIME25 的平均 pass@k 均最佳，领先基线 ≥1 分（8B 66.5、14B 72.6）。6) 消融：前缀统计量取 min 最优（58.3），优于几何平均 57.3、算术平均 54.0、累积乘积 52.7，说明"瓶颈信号"保留且避免截断饱和；额外基线 AsymRE 47.2、VESPO 55.1、SNIB 49.1 均低于 MinPRO 58.3（n=4）。7) 诊断：min 前缀因子均值 0.301/中位 0.258，23.95% ≤0.1；有效权重由首十分位 0.2502 降至末十分位 0.2036（≈18.6% 相对衰减），而 CISPO 恒为 ≈1，证实 MinPRO 对后续 token 施加了渐进衰减。

Conclusion: 结论：off-policy LLM 策略优化中，理论上严谨的重要性采样修正是前缀重要性比而非 token 级比；把 token 级松弛回退到前缀级信息可显著改善大滞后下的稳定性与峰值性能。MinPRO 以"前缀最小 token 比 × 当前 token 比"这一非累积、有偏但数值稳定的代理，既避免了全前缀累积乘积的方差爆炸与长度偏置，又保留了前缀级失配的下调信号，无需额外 critic 或复杂机制，训练开销可忽略，并可扩展到 MoE 大模型与极大策略滞后场景。局限：训练与评测仅覆盖数学推理（缺少公开的代码/逻辑推理 off-policy 数据集）；仅验证到 30B-A3B，未测试 100B 以上超大模型（算力需求高一个数量级以上）；诊断性统计缺乏 token 分母、精确 checkpoint 与不确定性估计，无法证明被衰减的 token 是否保留了有用的探索性推理。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has increasingly demonstrated strong ability to elicit reasoning behaviors in large language models (LLMs). For training efficiency, rollouts are typically generated in an off-policy manner using an older sampling policy and then used to update the current target policy. To correct the resulting discrepancy between the sampling and target policies, most existing RL objectives rely on a token-level importance sampling ratio, primarily due to its computational simplicity and numerical stability. However, we observe that token-level correction often leads to unstable training dynamics when the degree of off-policyness is large. In this paper, we revisit LLM policy optimization under off-policy conditions and show that the theoretically rigorous correction term is the prefix importance ratio, and that relaxing it to a token-level approximation can induce instability in RL post-training. To stabilize LLM optimization under large off-policy drift, we propose a simple yet effective objective, Minimum Prefix Ratio (MinPRO). MinPRO replaces the unstable cumulative prefix ratio with a non-cumulative surrogate based on the minimum token-level ratio observed in the preceding prefix. Extensive experiments on both dense and mixture-of-experts LLMs, across multiple mathematical reasoning benchmarks, demonstrate that MinPRO substantially improves training stability and peak performance in off-policy regimes.

</details>


### [249] [AutoRefine: From Trajectories to Reusable Expertise for Continual LLM Agent Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22758)
*Libin Qiu,Zhirong Gao,Junfu Chen,Yuhang Ye,Weizhi Huang,Xiaobo Xue,Wenkai Qiu,Shuo Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大规模语言模型智能体在积累经验方面存在问题，常将每个任务看作独立的挑战。现有方法以扁平化文本知识形式提取经验，无法捕捉复杂子任务的程序化逻辑，且缺乏维护机制导致经验库随经验积累而退化。作者提出了AutoRefine框架，从智能体执行历史中提取并维护双重形式的经验模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体缺乏有效的经验积累机制，常独立处理每个任务。最近的方法仅提取扁平化文本知识，无法捕捉复杂子任务的程序化逻辑，同时缺乏维护机制导致经验库退化。这限制了智能体在复杂任务上的长期学习和性能提升。

Method: AutoRefine框架从智能体执行历史中提取和维双重形式的经验模式。针对程序化子任务，提取具有独立推理和记忆能力的专用子智能体。针对静态知识，以指南或代码片段形式提取技能模式。通过持续维护机制（评分、剪枝和合并模式）防止经验库退化。

Result: 在ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和TravelPlanner三个测试集上，AutoRefine分别达到了98.4%、70.4%和27.1%的成功率，减少了20-73%的步骤数。在TravelPlanner上，自动提取超过了人工设计系统（27.1% vs 12.1%），展现了其捕捉程序化协调的能力。

Conclusion: AutoRefine框架在智能体经验积累方面取得了显著进展，通过从执行历史中提取双重形式的经验模式和持续的维护机制，能够有效捕捉程序化逻辑和静态知识，在多个测试集上展现出优异的性能和效率提升。

Abstract: Large language model agents often fail to accumulate knowledge from experience, treating each task as an independent challenge. Recent methods extract experience as flattened textual knowledge, which cannot capture procedural logic of complex subtasks. They also lack maintenance mechanisms, causing repository degradation as experience accumulates. We introduce AutoRefine, a framework that extracts and maintains dual-form Experience Patterns from agent execution histories. For procedural subtasks, we extract specialized subagents with independent reasoning and memory. For static knowledge, we extract skill patterns as guidelines or code snippets. A continuous maintenance mechanism scores, prunes, and merges patterns to prevent repository degradation. Evaluated on ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and TravelPlanner, AutoRefine achieves 98.4%, 70.4%, and 27.1% respectively, with 20-73% step reductions. On TravelPlanner, automatic extraction exceeds manually designed systems (27.1% vs 12.1%), demonstrating its ability to capture procedural coordination.

</details>


### [250] [TSPO: Breaking the Double Homogenization Dilemma in Multi-turn Search Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22776)
*Shichao Ma,Zhiyuan Ma,Ming Yang,Xiaofan Li,Xing Wu,Jintao Du,Yu Cheng,Weiqiang Wang,Qiliang Liu,Zhengyang Zhou,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TSPO（Turn-level Stage-aware Policy Optimization），面向多轮搜索增强推理的强化学习。其核心是 FOLR（First-Occurrence Latent Reward）机制：仅用标准 (x, a_gold) 监督，检测标准答案在检索反馈中首次出现的轮次 t*，对答案错误的轨迹在 t* 及其之前的轮次分配部分奖励 α，并在轮次级别做组内优势估计，从而同时缓解「过程级奖励同质化」与「组内奖励同质化」。在 7 个 QA 数据集上以 Qwen2.5-3B/7B 为基座，相对最强基线平均提升 24%（3B）与 13.6%（7B），且训练开销与 Search-R1 相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮工具调用推理的 RL 框架（Search-R1、MT-PPO 等）普遍采用稀疏的最终轮结果奖励（如 Exact Match 的 0/1 二值奖励），作者指出这会造成「双重同质化困境」（Double Homogenization Dilemma）：
(1) 过程级奖励同质化——整条多轮「思考—检索—工具调用—综合」轨迹被压缩为一个标量，检索成功但综合失败（Near-Miss, O-/P+）与完全失败（O-/P-）得到相同的 0 奖励，导致有效的检索行为被错误惩罚；
(2) 组内奖励同质化——在 GRPO 中，二值奖励使采样组容易全对或全错（实验中全错组占比持续 ≥40%），组内方差 σ=0 导致优势消失、梯度归零，浪费大量 near-miss 样本。
已有过程级奖励方案（PRM、ReasonRAG、StepSearch、MT-PPO 等）依赖昂贵人工标注、GPT-4 级评审模型或 MCTS 模拟，开销高、泛化差。作者希望用零额外监督的方式解决该困境。

Method: 1) 首次出现假设（First-Occurrence Hypothesis）与检验：在 51,713 条测试轨迹上建立「结果正确性 O × 证据存在性 P」的 2×2 列联表，Pearson 卡方统计量 χ²=5605.5（p<0.001），且所有 O+ 样本均满足 P+，验证「检索到标准答案是成功的必要条件」，据此把标准答案首次出现位置作为潜在过程信号。
2) FOLR 奖励分配：设 t* 为轨迹中首次出现 a_gold 的轮次，轮级奖励 r_{i,k}=1（最终答案正确，全轮赋满奖励）；=α（最终答案错误但 k≤t*）；=0（其余），α∈[0,1]。
3) 轮级优势估计：将 GRPO 的轨迹级标量奖励替换为逐轮奖励，在每个轮次 k 上对该轮组内全部 G 条轨迹的奖励做均值/标准差归一化得到 Â_{i,k}，再代入含重要性比与 clip 的 PPO 式目标（含 KL 惩罚）；变长轨迹按组内最大轮数用末轮奖励补齐，补齐轮在优化时被 mask。
4) 两种分配策略：All-groups（全部组使用轮级分配）与 All-wrong（仅全错组用轮级分配，其余沿用 GRPO 轨迹级）。
5) 实现基于 Search-R1 + Verl，8×A100，训练 500 步，G=5，总 batch 512，KL 系数 1e-3，熵系数 0。

Result: 主结果（EM）：Qwen2.5-7B 平均 0.444（vs ZeroSearch 0.391、Search-R1 0.385），相对最强基线 +13.6%；Qwen2.5-3B 平均 0.403（vs ZeroSearch 0.317、Search-R1 0.325），+24.0%；域内（NQ/HotpotQA）与域外（TriviaQA/PopQA/2Wiki/Musique/Bamboogle）均有增益；在 Musique 等难多跳数据集上优势尤为明显（如 7B 0.151 vs ZeroSearch 0.184 略低但优于多数基线，整体 3B 0.126）。
消融：组类型（None 0.254 → All groups 0.295 → All-wrong 0.300），仅处理全错组已足够且略优；α=0 退化为 GRPO 且性能最低，α=0.5 与 1.0 几乎无差异（组内归一化保序），说明任意 α>0 即足够。
训练动态：GRPO 策略熵快速坍缩至近零，TSPO 保持较高熵；KL 从 ~0.02 降至 ~0.01；梯度范数由 ~0.5 波动变为 ~0.25 平滑；奖励收敛更快更稳；响应长度由 2100+ 平稳压缩至 1700–1900 token，未出现为缩短而牺牲检索的过早截断。
成本：每步训练时间与 Search-R1 相当（3B：278.7s/268.6s vs 243.9s；7B：446.0s/402.3s vs 414.7s，7B 全错组策略甚至更低，因避免了冗余重复搜索）。

Conclusion: TSPO 通过「首次出现潜在奖励 + 轮级优势估计」以仅 (x, a_gold) 的极简监督同时破解过程级与组内奖励同质化，把原本被丢弃的 near-miss 轨迹转化为有效梯度信号，在 7 个 QA 基准上稳定超越 SOTA 稀疏奖励与稠密过程奖励基线，并保持训练效率与推理多样性的稳定（避免熵坍缩）。局限：FOLR 依赖检索正确性，无检索可解任务需重新定义奖励；实验仅覆盖搜索增强推理，向代码生成、多步工具调用等其他领域的迁移性仍待验证；未来方向是适配检索不完美/缺失场景并扩展任务类型。

Abstract: Multi-turn tool-integrated reasoning enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to solve complex tasks through iterative information retrieval. However, current reinforcement learning (RL) frameworks for search-augmented reasoning predominantly rely on sparse outcome-level rewards, leading to a "Double Homogenization Dilemma." This manifests as (1) Process homogenization, where the thinking, reasoning, and tooling involved in generation are ignored. (2) Intra-group homogenization, coarse-grained outcome rewards often lead to inefficiencies in intra-group advantage estimation with methods like Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) during sampling. To address this, we propose Turn-level Stage-aware Policy Optimization (TSPO). TSPO introduces the First-Occurrence Latent Reward (FOLR) mechanism, allocating partial rewards to the step where the ground-truth answer first appears, thereby preserving process-level signals and increasing reward variance within groups without requiring external reward models or any annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TSPO significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving average performance gains of 24% and 13.6% on Qwen2.5-3B and 7B models, respectively.

</details>


### [251] [CVeDRL: An Efficient Code Verifier via Difficulty-aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22803)
*Ji Shi,Peiming Guo,Meishan Zhang,Miao Zhang,Xuebo Liu,Min Zhang,Weili Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CVeDRL 提出一个面向"代码验证器（code verifier）"的强化学习训练框架：先从理论上推导出单元测试多数投票框架中"断言可靠性 p、分支覆盖率 c、测试套件数 M、候选程序数 N"之间的置信度下界，再据此设计「语法奖励 + 功能奖励」的 GRPO 基线模型，并进一步引入指数型奖励塑形（分支难度感知）与基于 Halstead 复杂度/可维护性指数的静态难度先验（样本难度感知）。最终仅用 0.6B 参数的 CVeDRL-0.6B 在 HumanEval+/MBPP+/LiveCodeBench 的代码验证任务上达到 SOTA，相比 SFT 基线 CodeRM-8B 推理吞吐提升 20 倍以上（7.08 vs 0.296 tok/kPar/s），并在单元测试质量上大幅降低错误率/失败率、提升通过率与分支覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 生成代码常需多次采样再由验证器筛选，验证器质量直接决定代码生成上限。现有 SFT 路线（CodeRM）存在三重瓶颈：① 缺乏大规模高质量单元测试标注数据，自动管线产出的测试中含大量错误用例；② 生成测试的错误率/失败率高，且需多次采样测试套件来缓解，带来严重的推理效率瓶颈；③ 推理成本高，难以作为轻量级验证器使用。RL 天然不需要标注、且可把通过率、覆盖率等执行反馈直接作为奖励，看似是更优范式；但作者的初步实验显示，仅使用粗粒度"功能正确性"奖励的朴素 RL 无法为困难分支与高难度样本生成有效单元测试——模型倾向于走 happy path、忽视边界条件。因此需要一套有理论依据的多维奖励设计与难度感知机制，回答"何时、如何用 RL 可靠地提升验证器性能"。

Method: 方法分四部分：（1）理论分析——在单元测试多数投票（best-of-N + 多测试套件）框架下做贝叶斯后验推导，得到单测试阈值 p > 1/(1+c)（c 为平均分支覆盖率）以及多套件 Hoeffding + 联合界形式的下界 p ≥ [1+√(2/M·ln((1−q)/(1−q′)·N))]/(1+c)，证明提升断言可靠性与覆盖率可同时放大选择置信度并降低所需测试套件数 M，为奖励设计提供依据；该界用于推导 M≈2ln(...) / [((1+c)p−1)²] 的可操作形式。（2）基线验证器（GRPO）——语法奖励 r_syn（生成内容须严格置于单个 Python 代码块内，AST 遍历到至少一个继承 unittest.TestCase 的类，正确 +1.0 / 错误 −1.0），用于规范格式、抑制 reward hacking；功能奖励 r_func（执行报错 −2.0；执行成功但断言不符 −1.5；通过则 +cov(u_i,s)），区分"幻觉错误"与"幻觉失败"两类失效；两者按"语法错只给语法奖励，否则相加"合成，并套用 GRPO 的组内标准化优势与 KL 约束目标。（3）分支难度感知（BDA）——将线性覆盖率奖励改为指数塑形 r_cov=(e^α−1)^{-1}[exp(α·cov)−1]，低覆盖区平缓、高覆盖区陡增，诱导模型探索稀疏到达的边界分支。（4）样本难度感知（SDA）——在执行前用静态分析给出难度先验：Halstead 复杂度 D_H（以训练集 95 分位数截断做 min–max 归一化）与可维护性难度 D_M=max(0,1−MI/100)（MI 由 Halstead 体积、圈复杂度、代码行数、注释比例构成），取几何平均 D=√(D_H·D_M) 强调"既难理解又难维护"的代码；最终奖励变为：报错 −2.0；失败 −1.0−(1−D)（软化惩罚）；通过 r_cov·(1+D)（困难样本给更大正反馈），使智能体在执行前即可区分"平凡失败"与"真正的困难片段"。训练用 verl 的 GRPO，Qwen3-0.6B 为基座，语料为 CodeRM-UnitTest（17,600 训练 / 59,600 测试项），2×A100 40GB 约 48 小时，学习率 1e-6、rollouts/prompt=2、KL 系数 0.001、clip 0.2。评测分两类：① 验证器性能（Vanilla / MBR-E / CodeT / CodeRM-8B / Llama3.1-70B / Qwen3-0.6B vs CVeDRL，在 HumanEval+、MBPP+、LiveCodeBench 上以 pass@N 选优）；② 单元测试质量（ER、FR、PR、BC、AN，在 HumanEval+、MBPP+、LeetCode 上直接执行生成测试）。

Result: ① 验证性能：CVeDRL-0.6B 在三个数据集上全面超越同规模基座 Qwen3-0.6B、CodeRM-8B 与 Llama3.1-70B。例如 MBPP+ 上作为 GPT-4o-mini 的奖励模型，pass@N 从 71.32% 提升到 76.93%（+5.61%）；LiveCodeBench 上 Llama3-8B 策略从 15.24% 提升到 16.75%。② 测试质量：MBPP+ 上 ER 0.53%、FR 15.79%、PR 83.68%、BC 97.37%、AN 仅 3.13 条断言——PR 比 GPT-4o 高约 16~18 个点、比 GPT-3.5 高 28.97 个点，LeetCode 上 BC 91.61%（比 GPT-3.5 高 15.08%），且断言数最少，说明能以最小测试集覆盖同等分支。③ 效率：Table 4 中 CVeDRL-0.6B 的 token 吞吐 7.083 tok/kPar/s，约为 CodeRM-8B（0.296）的 24 倍，平均时延 0.662 s/iter 低于 CodeRM 的 1.78 s/iter，能耗 246.2W 也最低；采样效率曲线显示仅需约 10 个测试用例即达到性能平台，显著优于需要 100 个用例的基线。④ 消融：SYN（语法奖励）主要降低错误率（ER 14.32→6.17）；BDA（指数塑形）主要提升分支覆盖（BC 89.79→96.75）；SDA（静态难度）主要提升通过率与错误率（ER 6.17→4.86、PR 69.47→79.96）；三者结合最优（MBPP+ ER 0.53 / PR 83.68 / BC 97.37）。同基座（Llama3.1-8B）对比中 CVeDRL-8B 明显优于 CodeRM-8B，说明收益来自方法而非规模。附录还表明 CoT 与额外条件分支提示反而降低通过率与覆盖，验证推理并非越多越好。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于把"代码验证器"从 SFT 数据工程问题转化为一个有理论支撑的 RL 奖励设计问题：通过置信度下界把"断言可靠性 + 分支覆盖率 + 难度"映射到可优化信号，再用语法/功能复合奖励、指数奖励塑形与静态复杂度先验把该信号落地，从而在 0.6B 规模上同时获得最佳验证精度与数量级的推理效率提升；理论界还给出"约 20 个测试套件即可达标、比低可靠度验证器省 3 倍以上采样"的量化指导。局限包括：仅支持完整可执行程序，无法验证补全等部分代码（需扩展到库级/多语言）；生成的测试擅长"排除错误解"但无法区分多个"正确解"（可引入变异测试或辅助原则）；受双盲评审限制模型权重暂未开源。后续方向为支持部分代码验证、跨语言与更广生态的库级测试训练，以及引入变异测试解决"正确性不可分辨"问题。

Abstract: Code verifiers play a critical role in post-verification for LLM-based code generation, yet existing supervised fine-tuning methods suffer from data scarcity, high failure rates, and poor inference efficiency. While reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising alternative by optimizing models through execution-driven rewards without labeled supervision, our preliminary results show that naive RL with only functionality rewards fails to generate effective unit tests for difficult branches and samples. We first theoretically analyze showing that branch coverage, sample difficulty, syntactic and functional correctness can be jointly modeled as RL rewards, where optimizing these signals can improve the reliability of unit-test-based verification. Guided by this analysis, we design syntax- and functionality-aware rewards and further propose branch- and sample-difficulty--aware RL using exponential reward shaping and static analysis metrics. With this formulation, CVeDRL achieves state-of-the-art performance with only 0.6B parameters, yielding up to 28.97% higher pass rate and 15.08% higher branch coverage than GPT-3.5, while delivering over $20\times$ faster inference than competitive baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/LIGHTCHASER1/CVeDRL.git

</details>


### [252] [Learning with Challenges: Adaptive Difficulty-Aware Data Generation for Mobile GUI Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22781)
*Linjia Kang,Zhimin Wang,Yongkang Zhang,Duo Wu,Jinghe Wang,Ming Ma,Haopeng Yan,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileGen是一种自适应匹配GUI智能体能力边界的数据生成框架，通过解耦任务难度为结构和语义维度，迭代评估智能体能力并采样目标难度，生成高质量交互轨迹以提高训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动GUI智能体训练数据生成方法依赖人工演示或自动探索，缺乏对任务难度的细粒度控制，导致训练难度与智能体能力不匹配，限制了学习效果。受人类通过渐进式挑战学习技能的启发，需要开发能自适应对齐训练难度与智能体能力边界的数据生成框架。

Method: 1）将任务难度明确解耦为结构维度（轨迹长度）和语义维度（任务目标）；2）在精选先验数据集上迭代评估智能体，构建其在两个维度上的能力边界系统画像；3）自适应计算任务难度概率分布，采样下一轮训练的目标难度；4）基于采样难度，使用多智能体可控生成器合成高质量交互轨迹及相应任务指令。

Result: 在多个挑战性基准测试中，MobileGen持续优于现有数据生成方法，将GUI智能体的平均性能提高了1.57倍，表明能力对齐的数据生成对移动GUI智能体训练至关重要。

Conclusion: MobileGen通过自适应对齐训练难度与GUI智能体能力边界，实现了更有效的数据生成，显著提升了智能体性能，为移动GUI智能体训练提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale, high-quality interaction trajectories are essential for advancing mobile Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents. While existing methods typically rely on labor-intensive human demonstrations or automated model exploration to generate GUI trajectories, they lack fine-grained control over task difficulty. This fundamentally restricts learning effectiveness due to the mismatch between the training difficulty and the agent's capabilities. Inspired by how humans acquire skills through progressively challenging tasks, we propose MobileGen, a novel data generation framework that adaptively aligns training difficulty with the GUI agent's capability frontier. Specifically, MobileGen explicitly decouples task difficulty into structural (e.g., trajectory length) and semantic (e.g., task goal) dimensions. It then iteratively evaluates the agent on a curated prior dataset to construct a systematic profile of its capability frontier across these two dimensions. With this profile, the probability distribution of task difficulty is adaptively computed, from which the target difficulty for the next round of training can be sampled. Guided by the sampled difficulty, a multi-agent controllable generator is finally used to synthesize high-quality interaction trajectories along with corresponding task instructions. Extensive experiments show that MobileGen consistently outperforms existing data generation methods by improving the average performance of GUI agents by 1.57 times across multiple challenging benchmarks. This highlights the importance of capability-aligned data generation for effective mobile GUI agent training.

</details>


### [253] [TriCEGAR: A Trace-Driven Abstraction Mechanism for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22997)
*Roham Koohestani,Ateş Görpelioğlu,Egor Klimov,Burcu Kulahcioglu Ozkan,Maliheh Izadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TriCEGAR：基于轨迹的抽象机制，自动化构建智能体行为MDP以支持动态概率保证，通过谓词树学习和反例精化降低验证采用门槛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态概率保证(DPA)方法需手动定义状态抽象，导致验证与具体应用启发式方法耦合且增加采用难度。需要自动化状态构造机制来支持智能体AI系统的运行时验证

Method: 提出TriCEGAR方法：1) 从执行日志中自动化构建状态抽象，学习谓词树表示；2) 利用反例精化抽象；3) 在线构建智能体行为MDP；4) 通过概率模型检验计算界限概率；5) 利用运行可能性进行异常检测

Result: 开发了框架原生实现，捕获类型化智能体生命周期事件，构建抽象并从构建MDP，支持计算Pmax(success)和Pmin(failure)等界限概率，展示运行可能性可用于护栏信号

Conclusion: TriCEGAR通过自动化状态抽象解决了DPA方法的关键限制，降低了智能体AI系统运行时验证的采用门槛，为智能体行为保证提供了实用的自动化解决方案

Abstract: Agentic AI systems act through tools and evolve their behavior over long, stochastic interaction traces. This setting complicates assurance, because behavior depends on nondeterministic environments and probabilistic model outputs. Prior work introduced runtime verification for agentic AI via Dynamic Probabilistic Assurance (DPA), learning an MDP online and model checking quantitative properties. A key limitation is that developers must manually define the state abstraction, which couples verification to application-specific heuristics and increases adoption friction. This paper proposes TriCEGAR, a trace-driven abstraction mechanism that automates state construction from execution logs and supports online construction of an agent behavioral MDP. TriCEGAR represents abstractions as predicate trees learned from traces and refined using counterexamples. We describe a framework-native implementation that (i) captures typed agent lifecycle events, (ii) builds abstractions from traces, (iii) constructs an MDP, and (iv) performs probabilistic model checking to compute bounds such as Pmax(success) and Pmin(failure). We also show how run likelihoods enable anomaly detection as a guardrailing signal.

</details>


### [254] [Toward IIT-Inspired Consciousness in LLMs: A Reward-Based Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22786)
*Hamid Reza Akbari,Mohammad Hossein Sameti,Amir M. Mansourian,Mohammad Hossein Rohban,Hossein Sameti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于整合信息理论(IIT)的奖励学习框架，使语言模型生成更简练的文本，在保持准确率的同时长度减少最高达31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 追求人工通用智能(AGI)是语言模型发展的核心目标，意识样处理可能成为关键促进因素。虽然当前语言模型不具有意识，但表现出类似某些意识方面的行为。研究旨在将领先的意识理论——整合信息理论(IIT)——通过奖励学习范式融入语言模型。

Method: 借鉴IIT的核心原则，设计了一种新颖的奖励函数，用于量化文本的因果性、连贯性和整合性——这些特征与意识处理相关。通过优化这一IIT启发的奖励，在语言模型中实现更简练的文本生成。

Result: 实证结果表明，优化IIT启发的奖励能显著减少生成文本长度。在领域外任务中，通过精细调参可实现输出长度减少最高达31%，同时保持与基础模型相当的准确率水平。此外，该训练方法还能改善模型信心校准和测试时计算扩展性分析。

Conclusion: 提出的框架具有重要实用优势：概念简单、计算高效、无需外部数据或辅助模型，且利用通用的基于能力的信号而非任务特定启发式方法。代码已开源。

Abstract: The pursuit of Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) is a central goal in language model development, in which consciousness-like processing could serve as a key facilitator. While current language models are not conscious, they exhibit behaviors analogous to certain aspects of consciousness. This paper investigates the implementation of a leading theory of consciousness, Integrated Information Theory (IIT), within language models via a reward-based learning paradigm. IIT provides a formal, axiom-based mathematical framework for quantifying consciousness. Drawing inspiration from its core principles, we formulate a novel reward function that quantifies a text's causality, coherence and integration, characteristics associated with conscious processing. Empirically, it is found that optimizing for this IIT-inspired reward leads to more concise text generation. On out of domain tasks, careful tuning achieves up to a 31% reduction in output length while preserving accuracy levels comparable to the base model. In addition to primary task performance, the broader effects of this training methodology on the model's confidence calibration and test-time computational scaling is analyzed. The proposed framework offers significant practical advantages: it is conceptually simple, computationally efficient, requires no external data or auxiliary models, and leverages a general, capability-driven signal rather than task-specific heuristics. Code available at https://github.com/MH-Sameti/LLM_PostTraining.git

</details>


### [255] [Conditional Performance Guarantee for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22790)
*Jianguo Huang,Hao Zeng,Bingyi Jing,Hongxin Wei,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出G-PAC推理框架，通过输入空间分组实现群体级别的PAC保证，在保持计算效率的同时提供群体条件风险控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型虽然通过链式思维推理表现出色，但计算成本高昂。现有的PAC推理框架只在边际情况下提供统计保证，无法提供精确的条件覆盖

Method: 提出G-PAC推理框架：1) Group PAC (G-PAC)：针对已知群体结构，通过输入空间划分；2) Clustered PAC (C-PAC)：针对未知分组，自动学习群体结构。两种方法都通过自适应切换思考与非思考模型来实现

Result: 理论证明G-PAC和C-PAC都能实现群体条件风险控制，在异质环境中分组策略能严格提高效率。多基准实验表明两种方法都能成功实现群体条件风险控制，同时保持显著的计算节省

Conclusion: G-PAC框架提供了一种实用的群体级别PAC推理方法，在保证统计可靠性的同时大幅提升计算效率，为解决大规模推理模型的计算成本问题提供了有效方案

Abstract: Large reasoning models have shown strong performance through extended chain-of-thought reasoning, yet their computational cost remains significant. Probably approximately correct (PAC) reasoning provides statistical guarantees for efficient reasoning by adaptively switching between thinking and non-thinking models, but the guarantee holds only in the marginal case and does not provide exact conditional coverage. We propose G-PAC reasoning, a practical framework that provides PAC-style guarantees at the group level by partitioning the input space. We develop two instantiations: Group PAC (G-PAC) reasoning for known group structures and Clustered PAC (C-PAC) reasoning for unknown groupings. We prove that both G-PAC and C-PAC achieve group-conditional risk control, and that grouping can strictly improve efficiency over marginal PAC reasoning in heterogeneous settings. Our experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that G-PAC and C-PAC successfully achieve group-conditional risk control while maintaining substantial computational savings.

</details>


### [256] [Aligning the Unseen in Attributed Graphs: Interplay between Graph Geometry and Node Attributes Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22806)
*Aldric Labarthe,Roland Bouffanais,Julien Randon-Furling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出 attributed graph 上的联合表示学习（同时重构节点属性与图结构）在几何上是错误设定的：它把属性流形的黎曼度量与图核诱导的扩散度量强行"对齐"，从而抹掉了图与属性之间的几何失配信息。作者提出一个两阶段训练的 VAE 诊断探针：第一阶段只从属性学习属性流形，第二阶段冻结编码器、仅通过解码器 Jacobian 对度量张量做局部形变，使属性流形上的 Heat Kernel 去匹配邻接矩阵；形变所需的量被转化为链接级/节点级的可解释结构描述子 Z_ij 与 S_i，从而可检测异配（heterophily）连通与结构异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 attributed graph 表示学习通常同时优化属性重构与结构重构（如 VGAE、多解码器架构），等价于假设属性几何与图拓扑是兼容的（同配过程）。作者指出这在理论上存在关键缺陷：(1) 图连通性本身隐含几何（随机几何图理论），而点云具有内在流形，二者可能是不可通约的度量空间；(2) 在真实存在异配性的网络中，属性几何与连通性显著偏离，现有模型把这种偏离当作可平滑掉的噪声/重构误差，系统性地丢弃了关于图生成过程的信息；(3) 引入可学习映射 f(z) 吸收度量差异的做法存在根本性识别问题——数据内在几何与解码器引入的任意扭曲不可区分，且对非欧拓扑（双曲性）无法完全解决；论文主张反向审视：把"几何失配"形式化并度量，将其从优化障碍转为可解释的结构信号（尤其对社区发现与异常检测）。

Method: 问题形式化：属性解码器 M 在隐空间诱导拉回黎曼度量 g_M，结构核 K 经 Moore–Aronszajn 定理诱导 RKHS 与度量张量 g^K_ij = ∂²K/∂u_i∂v_j，两者在同一流形上竞争（Problem 1）。核的选择决定失配的性质：内积核强制平坦欧氏几何（g^K=Id），双线性核给出曲率权衡，而 Heat Kernel 在短时极限下由 Varadhan 渐近式收敛到 g_M，在大时标下变为扩散度量（紧流形上为微分同胚，可被解码器反演），但仍强加了"结构连通=属性邻近"的同配归纳偏置——正因如此，残差失配才可以量化同配程度。
方法为定制的两阶段 VAE：Phase 1（流形学习）仅用属性重构（高斯 NLL + 两项方差匹配惩罚 + KL），估计属性几何 g_M = (J^μ)^T J^μ + (J^σ)^T J^σ，并由 Varadhan 公式在属性测地距离上构造多尺度热核 H（时间尺度由图拉普拉斯特征值确定：t_min=1/λ_max、t_max=4/λ_2，取 k=15 个对数间隔尺度）。Phase 2（几何对齐）冻结编码器 φ 固定隐坐标，把重构损失替换为核对齐目标 L₂ = ‖A − K_M(θ)‖²₂，迫使解码器局部形变度量张量去匹配图的扩散几何，从而避免标准图嵌入通过重排隐编码平凡地聚类相邻节点。
关键技术是可微分测地距离：低维（d≤3）用均匀网格离散流形，缓存度量张量，以中点法求边权后用 Dijkstra 求最短路，并将"路径选择"与"权重求值"解耦（路径 detach，仅沿路径权重反传梯度），实现端到端几何正则；高维用线性插值估计器（沿隐空间线段积分度量，梯形积分），作为绕开维度灾难的回退方案。
最终把几何冲突转为结构描述子：链接级 Z_ij 为 Phase 1 与 Phase 2 配对距离变化的对数差的中位数绝对偏差归一化 Z 分数，节点级 S_i = Σ_{j≠i} Z_ij。

Result: 合成数据（Swiss-roll 型 2 维流形嵌入 R^20，N=500，用高斯相似度核生成偏好连接网络，并打乱 70 个节点组内得分制造异配扰动）：Phase 1 估计测地距离与理论值高度一致（R²=0.894 Dijkstra / 0.889 线性插值，Spearman ρ=0.85/0.84，夹角 ρ=0.832）；线性插值估计器与精确数值解近乎一致（r=0.999、ρ=0.999、误差 1.0%、偏差 +0.015）。以 S_i 作异常分数，社区检测 F1=82.50%（线性插值版本 83.75%）、ROC AUC=96.48%、ARI=73.13%，显著优于谱聚类（36.65%）、Louvain（44.87%）、VAE+K-Means/Isolation Forest（45.60%/45.71%），并超过 VGAE 重构误差基线（74.29%，与 DOMINANT 类方法相当）。Phase 2 显示流形整体被平滑且"曲率跌落线"被抹除，而 C 形空腔区（无信息，源于数据缺失导致的解码器方差）与两类分界邻域发生显著改变；Z_ij 相比余弦同配性提供了真正的链接级度量（与同配性相关 r=0.3193、ρ=0.3288、MI=0.2275）。
真实数据（法国法兰西岛公共交通网络：INSEE 20 项市镇社会经济指标 + GTFS 构造的 N=1,266 节点加权图）：Phase 1 隐空间形成 5 个被"曲率墙"分隔的功能区（代理决策树解释为：都会中心、密集城市、产业节点、居住外围、农业/低活跃基线）。Phase 2 的集群交互图显示都会中心（Cluster 3）是几何张力主源（内部平均 Z=+16.2，且与密集城市、产业节点连边强正），对应公共交通制造了连接社会经济差异区域的"捷径"；而农业基线与居住外围以负 Z 为主（-3.8、-2.7），量化了外围缺乏横向连通（banlieue-to-banlieue）的可达性缺口，与"放射状/星形"大巴黎 RER/Metro 拓扑的批评一致。直接把交通便利度回归到属性或两两相似度几乎无解释力（R²=0.003 / 0.0031），反证信息确实藏在异质的几何失配中。

Conclusion: 核心论点是"几何失配是特征而非 bug"：现有联合嵌入架构在数学上是错误设定的——它们强制度量等价而非建模度量张力，因而系统性地丢弃了可解释的结构化信号；论文主张转向显式推理几何不相容性的架构。方法定位为诊断性探针（故意限制模型灵活性以暴露矛盾），而非可扩展的预测架构：以处理百万级节点的能力换取了精确结构信号的可解释性，适用于中等规模图或大系统的子采样区域，类似谱/扩散类探索方法。主要局限与未来方向是几何深度学习工具链的关键缺口——缺乏可扩展的可微分测地距离估计器（现有事后分析多依赖非可微求解器），而训练端几何正则要求梯度必须穿过距离计算本身。

Abstract: The standard approach to representation learning on attributed graphs -- i.e., simultaneously reconstructing node attributes and graph structure -- is geometrically flawed, as it merges two potentially incompatible metric spaces. This forces a destructive alignment that erodes information about the graph's underlying generative process. To recover this lost signal, we introduce a custom variational autoencoder that separates manifold learning from structural alignment. By quantifying the metric distortion needed to map the attribute manifold onto the graph's Heat Kernel, we transform geometric conflict into an interpretable structural descriptor. Experiments show our method uncovers connectivity patterns and anomalies undetectable by conventional approaches, proving both their theoretical inadequacy and practical limitations.

</details>


### [257] [Game-Theoretic Co-Evolution for LLM-Based Heuristic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22896)
*Xinyi Ke,Kai Li,Junliang Xing,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ASRO框架将启发式发现重构为求解器与实例生成器的程序级协同进化，通过博弈论和LLM驱动的最佳响应预言实现动态对抗训练，显著改善静态评估方法的过拟合和泛化能力不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动启发式发现方法主要依赖静态评估，在固定实例分布上训练容易导致过拟合，对分布偏移的泛化能力差，需要更动态、自适应的训练机制。

Method: 提出了算法空间响应预言框架，将启发式发现建模为求解器与实例生成器的零和博弈，维护双方不断增长的战略池，通过LLM驱动的最佳响应预言对抗对手的混合元策略，实现自生成课程的自适应进化。

Result: 在多个组合优化领域，ASRO均显著优于基于相同程序搜索机制的静态训练基线方法，在多样化和分布外实例上展现出更好的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将启发式发现重构为程序级的协同进化博弈，通过自适应的对抗训练替代静态评估，能够有效提升方法在分布偏移下的泛化性能，为自动启发式发现提供了新的动态训练范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled rapid progress in automatic heuristic discovery (AHD), yet most existing methods are predominantly limited by static evaluation against fixed instance distributions, leading to potential overfitting and poor generalization under distributional shifts. We propose Algorithm Space Response Oracles (ASRO), a game-theoretic framework that reframes heuristic discovery as a program level co-evolution between solver and instance generator. ASRO models their interaction as a two-player zero-sum game, maintains growing strategy pools on both sides, and iteratively expands them via LLM-based best-response oracles against mixed opponent meta-strategies, thereby replacing static evaluation with an adaptive, self-generated curriculum. Across multiple combinatorial optimization domains, ASRO consistently outperforms static-training AHD baselines built on the same program search mechanisms, achieving substantially improved generalization and robustness on diverse and out-of-distribution instances.

</details>


### [258] [MulFeRL: Enhancing Reinforcement Learning with Verbal Feedback in a Multi-turn Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22900)
*Xuancheng Li,Haitao Li,Yujia Zhou,YiqunLiu,Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MulFeRL 是一个多轮、事件触发的 RLVR 框架：它只在"整组 rollout 全部失败"（组内零奖励对比、GRPO 优势值恒为 0）的低信号样本上触发语言反馈，用反馈作为条件引导重新采样，再把反馈带来的"验证器确认的进步"转化为可优化的训练信号——组内仍有对比时用 GRPO，整组由全失败变为全成功时用新提出的 Feedback-Contrastive Optimization (FCO) 做跨状态成对对比学习。反馈被固定注入推理模板中的 <feedback> 槽位，注入 token 在似然计算中被掩码。模型在 OpenR1-Math 的 4k 样本上训练，在数学与科学/通用推理基准上同时超越 SFT、自蒸馏与 RLVR 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 用验证器给出 0/1 标量奖励，但奖励稀疏且信息量低：失败样本只得到"错误"这一结论，不指出错在哪一步，导致信用分配差、梯度噪声大、样本效率低、训练进入平台期。理论分析（命题1）表明当组内 K 条 rollout 奖励一致时，GRPO 组内归一化优势恒为 0，奖励相关项完全失效；而困难题目恰恰最易产生"全失败组"（P_Failed ≈ 1−K·p），即最需要学习的样本恰恰得不到学习信号。已有利用语言反馈的方法要么把反馈压成粗粒度奖励标签或模仿目标（丢失细粒度诊断信息），要么用特权教师做自蒸馏（过度压缩不确定性），要么把反馈无差别分配到所有样本（而成功样本本已被结果奖励充分区分，反馈被浪费）。核心问题因此是：如何把语言反馈转化为在失败样本上仍能驱动 RL 的稳定学习信号？

Method: 框架分两阶段、共三个机制。（1）事件触发的 progress induction：为每个 prompt 定义奖励状态 z ∈ {Solved, Contrastive, Failed}；仅当组处于 Failed 状态时才调用反馈提供者 F_ψ 生成组级反馈 f（先对 K 条 rollout 分 g 个子组各自生成反馈再合并为一条，格式为 Issue / Fix steps，禁止给出最终答案），把反馈注入固定 <feedback> 槽位得到新上下文 c^(t+1)=Inject(x, f)，重新采样并验证，直至出现 Contrastive/Solved 或耗尽 turn 预算。（2）progress credit assignment：Contrastive 组用标准 clipped GRPO（同上下文内成功/失败对比）；Failed→Solved 跃迁用 FCO，即以长度归一化的参考归一化得分 s_θ(c,y)=|y|^{-1}[log π_θ−log π_ref] 构造跨上下文成对损失 −log σ(τ[D_θ(y⁺,y⁻)−γ])，其中 D_θ 为跨上下文得分差，正样本来自当前全成功组、负样本来自紧邻的上一轮全失败组，掩码掉注入的反馈 token，并用随机置换采样 K 个配对（证明为全 K² 对平均的无偏估计）。反馈文本本身永不被直接优化，只改变采样条件，更新始终由验证器门控。（3）结构化反馈注入：借鉴 RAG 的 passage injection，把反馈放在 <thinking> 内的固定 <feedback> 槽位（第 0 轮为空），使反馈 token 可被识别、掩码与规范化；奖励 1 需同时满足答案正确与 schema 合规。实现上基于 verl，K=8、T=3 轮（1 初始 + 最多 2 次再生成）、temperature=1、反馈上限 1k、回答上限 8k，GPT-4o 充当反馈模拟器，λ_FCO=0.01、τ=0.005。

Result: 主结果（Pass@1，5 次运行均值）：Qwen2.5-7B-Base 上 MulFeRL 平均 46.26%，优于最强基线 Critique-GRPO 42.83%（AIME24 24.00 vs 20.67，Minerva 37.87 vs 33.82）；Qwen3-4B-Inst 上平均 68.36%，优于 Critique-GRPO 63.65%（AMC23 90.50、AIME24 68.00、TheoremQA 55.75），且在 MMLU-Pro/GPQA-Diamond/TheoremQA 等 OOD 任务上同步提升，说明存在跨域迁移而非单纯拟合训练域。消融（Qwen2.5-7B-Base）：去掉反馈再生成退化最大（AIME24 −8.0、MATH500 −7.9），去掉 FCO 次之（AIME24 −4.3），把固定槽位注入换成普通提示也有小幅下降（−3.3/−1.0），三个组件均有贡献。控制实验表明优势并非来自输出格式或单纯调用 GPT-4o：统一输出格式后 GRPO/Critique-GRPO 反而略降；在 GPT-4o API 成本大致相同的情况下，GPT-4o 蒸馏基线仍低于 MulFeRL。模拟器消融显示性能随模拟器质量递增（Qwen3-1.7B 61.32 → 自反馈 65.62 → GPT-4o-mini 66.84 → GPT-4o 68.36）。反馈质量消融显示纯二元判定反馈明显弱于完整语言反馈，错误判定（wrong-verdict）会掉点，结构化注入可降低对误导性反馈的敏感度。测试时多轮反馈下 MulFeRL 在每个轮次均最优，且能更好地利用外部反馈。代价方面，MulFeRL 每步 GPU 成本高于单轮 RL 基线（早期失败多、再生成频繁），但收敛更快；turn 预算从 0 增至 2 收益明显，继续增加迅速饱和，故建议小到中等 turn 预算、提前停止与退火式再生成。

Conclusion: MulFeRL 说明语言反馈本身不是奖励，但可以作为条件干预使失败样本产生验证器可确认的进步，从而被转化为梯度信号；其统一原则是"反馈改变采样条件，信用分配只由验证器判定的对比来做"，并按对比出现的位置路由到 GRPO（组内对比）或 FCO（跨状态对比）。理论部分给出的是局部/条件性的结论（局部梯度方向、逃逸概率、FCO 置换采样无偏性），而非全局单调改进保证。局限：多轮再生成带来额外解码与验证开销，且依赖可靠验证与有效反馈；目前仅使用 rollout 级反馈与保守触发策略（仅对全失败组触发、简单配对规则），可能忽略部分正确或接近正确样本中的细粒度改进；未来可扩展到步骤级/过程级反馈、自适应触发和更丰富的信用分配方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is widely used to improve reasoning in multiple domains, yet outcome-only scalar rewards are often sparse and uninformative, especially on failed samples, where they merely indicate failure and provide no insight into why the reasoning fails. In this paper, we investigate how to leverage richer verbal feedback to guide RLVR training on failed samples, and how to convert such feedback into a trainable learning signal. Specifically, we propose a multi-turn feedback-guided reinforcement learning framework. It builds on three mechanisms: (1) dynamic multi-turn regeneration guided by feedback, triggered only on failed samples, (2) two complementary learning signals for within-turn and cross-turn optimization, and (3) structured feedback injection into the model's reasoning process. Trained on sampled OpenR1-Math, the approach outperforms supervised fine-tuning and RLVR baselines in-domain and generalizes well out-of-domain.

</details>


### [259] [Alignment among Language, Vision and Action Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22948)
*Nicola Milano,Stefano Nolfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A fundamental question in cognitive science and AI concerns whether different learning modalities: language, vision, and action, give rise to distinct or shared internal representations. Traditional views assume that models trained on different data types develop specialized, non-transferable representations. However, recent evidence suggests unexpected convergence: models optimized for distinct tasks may develop similar representational geometries. We investigate whether this convergence extends to embodied action learning by training a transformer-based agent to execute goal-directed behaviors in response to natural language instructions. Using behavioral cloning on the BabyAI platform, we generated action-grounded language embeddings shaped exclusively by sensorimotor control requirements. We then compared these representations with those extracted from state-of-the-art large language models (LLaMA, Qwen, DeepSeek, BERT) and vision-language models (CLIP, BLIP). Despite substantial differences in training data, modality, and objectives, we observed robust cross-modal alignment. Action representations aligned strongly with decoder-only language models and BLIP (precision@15: 0.70-0.73), approaching the alignment observed among language models themselves. Alignment with CLIP and BERT was significantly weaker. These findings indicate that linguistic, visual, and action representations converge toward partially shared semantic structures, supporting modality-independent semantic organization and highlighting potential for cross-domain transfer in embodied AI systems.

### [260] [EvoClinician: A Self-Evolving Agent for Multi-Turn Medical Diagnosis via Test-Time Evolutionary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22964)
*Yufei He,Juncheng Liu,Zhiyuan Hu,Yulin Chen,Yue Liu,Yuan Sui,Yibo Li,Nuo Chen,Jun Hu,Bryan Hooi,Xinxing Xu,Jiang Bian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Prevailing medical AI operates on an unrealistic ''one-shot'' model, diagnosing from a complete patient file. However, real-world diagnosis is an iterative inquiry where Clinicians sequentially ask questions and order tests to strategically gather information while managing cost and time. To address this, we first propose Med-Inquire, a new benchmark designed to evaluate an agent's ability to perform multi-turn diagnosis. Built upon a dataset of real-world clinical cases, Med-Inquire simulates the diagnostic process by hiding a complete patient file behind specialized Patient and Examination agents. They force the agent to proactively ask questions and order tests to gather information piece by piece. To tackle the challenges posed by Med-Inquire, we then introduce EvoClinician, a self-evolving agent that learns efficient diagnostic strategies at test time. Its core is a ''Diagnose-Grade-Evolve'' loop: an Actor agent attempts a diagnosis; a Process Grader agent performs credit assignment by evaluating each action for both clinical yield and resource efficiency; finally, an Evolver agent uses this feedback to update the Actor's strategy by evolving its prompt and memory. Our experiments show EvoClinician outperforms continual learning baselines and other self-evolving agents like memory agents. The code is available at https://github.com/yf-he/EvoClinician

Our experiments show EvoClinician outperforms continual learning baselines and other self-evolving agents like memory agents. The code is available at https://github.com/yf-he/EvoClinician

### [261] [Golden Goose: A Simple Trick to Synthesize Unlimited RLVR Tasks from Unverifiable Internet Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22975)
*Ximing Lu,David Acuna,Jaehun Jung,Jian Hu,Di Zhang,Shizhe Diao,Yunheng Zou,Shaokun Zhang,Brandon Cui,Mingjie Liu,Hyunwoo Kim,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a cornerstone for unlocking complex reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs). Yet, scaling up RL is bottlenecked by limited existing verifiable data, where improvements increasingly saturate over prolonged training. To overcome this, we propose Golden Goose, a simple trick to synthesize unlimited RLVR tasks from unverifiable internet text by constructing a multiple-choice question-answering version of the fill-in-the-middle task. Given a source text, we prompt an LLM to identify and mask key reasoning steps, then generate a set of diverse, plausible distractors. This enables us to leverage reasoning-rich unverifiable corpora typically excluded from prior RLVR data construction (e.g., science textbooks) to synthesize GooseReason-0.7M, a large-scale RLVR dataset with over 0.7 million tasks spanning mathematics, programming, and general scientific domains. Empirically, GooseReason effectively revives models saturated on existing RLVR data, yielding robust, sustained gains under continuous RL and achieving new state-of-the-art results for 1.5B and 4B-Instruct models across 15 diverse benchmarks. Finally, we deploy Golden Goose in a real-world setting, synthesizing RLVR tasks from raw FineWeb scrapes for the cybersecurity domain, where no prior RLVR data exists. Training Qwen3-4B-Instruct on the resulting data GooseReason-Cyber sets a new state-of-the-art in cybersecurity, surpassing a 7B domain-specialized model with extensive domain-specific pre-training and post-training. This highlights the potential of automatically scaling up RLVR data by exploiting abundant, reasoning-rich, unverifiable internet text.

Yet, scaling up RL is bottlenecked by limited existing verifiable data, where improvements increasingly saturate over prolonged training. Empirically, GooseReason effectively revives models saturated on existing RLVR data, yielding robust, sustained gains under continuous RL and achieving new state-of-the-art results for 1.5B and 4B-Instruct models across 15 diverse benchmarks. ### [262] [Quantifying Model Uniqueness in Heterogeneous AI Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22977)
*Lei You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于干预的实验设计统计框架（ISQED），通过量化模型行为中不可被同类表达的残差（PIER）来审计模型生态系统的独特性，突破仅观测日志的局限性，实现高效采样并揭示传统合作博弈方法的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统从孤立预测器演变为复杂的、异构的基础模型与专用适配器生态系统，如何区分真正的行为新颖性与功能冗余成为关键治理挑战。需要超越单一模型解释，建立基于干预的异构模型生态系统审计与治理科学。

Method: 提出In-Silico Quasi-Experimental Design (ISQED)框架，通过强制跨模型的匹配干预来隔离内在模型身份，量化独特性为Peer-Inexpressible Residual (PIER)，即目标行为中严格不可被任何随机凸组合所表达的成分。开发了DISCO (Design-Integrated Synthetic Control)估计器，并设计了自适应查询协议以实现最小最大最优样本效率。

Result: 理论证明观测日志存在根本局限性：无干预控制下独特性在数学上不可识别。推导了主动审计的缩放规律，自适应查询协议达到最优样本效率（dσ²γ⁻²log(Nd/δ)）。实证表明合作博弈论方法（如Shapley值）本质上无法检测冗余。框架在计算机视觉模型（ResNet/ConvNeXt/ViT）、大规模语言模型（BERT/RoBERTa）和城市规模交通预测器等多种生态系统中成功部署。

Conclusion: 该研究将可信AI从解释单一模型扩展到建立基于干预的异构模型生态系统审计与治理原则科学。ISQED框架为区分真实行为新颖性与功能冗余提供了理论基础和实践工具，对AI生态系统的监管具有重要意义。

Abstract: As AI systems evolve from isolated predictors into complex, heterogeneous ecosystems of foundation models and specialized adapters, distinguishing genuine behavioral novelty from functional redundancy becomes a critical governance challenge. Here, we introduce a statistical framework for auditing model uniqueness based on In-Silico Quasi-Experimental Design (ISQED). By enforcing matched interventions across models, we isolate intrinsic model identity and quantify uniqueness as the Peer-Inexpressible Residual (PIER), i.e. the component of a target's behavior strictly irreducible to any stochastic convex combination of its peers, with vanishing PIER characterizing when such a routing-based substitution becomes possible. We establish the theoretical foundations of ecosystem auditing through three key contributions. First, we prove a fundamental limitation of observational logs: uniqueness is mathematically non-identifiable without intervention control. Second, we derive a scaling law for active auditing, showing that our adaptive query protocol achieves minimax-optimal sample efficiency ($dσ^2γ^{-2}\log(Nd/δ)$). Third, we demonstrate that cooperative game-theoretic methods, such as Shapley values, fundamentally fail to detect redundancy. We implement this framework via the DISCO (Design-Integrated Synthetic Control) estimator and deploy it across diverse ecosystems, including computer vision models (ResNet/ConvNeXt/ViT), large language models (BERT/RoBERTa), and city-scale traffic forecasters. These results move trustworthy AI beyond explaining single models: they establish a principled, intervention-based science of auditing and governing heterogeneous model ecosystems.

</details>


### [263] [Why Your Deep Research Agent Fails? On Hallucination Evaluation in Full Research Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22984)
*Yuhao Zhan,Tianyu Fan,Linxuan Huang,Zirui Guo,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Diagnosing the failure mechanisms of Deep Research Agents (DRAs) remains a critical challenge. Existing benchmarks predominantly rely on end-to-end evaluation, obscuring critical intermediate hallucinations, such as flawed planning, that accumulate throughout the research trajectory. To bridge this gap, we propose a shift from outcome-based to process-aware evaluation by auditing the full research trajectory. We introduce the PIES Taxonomy to categorize hallucinations along functional components (Planning vs. Summarization) and error properties (Explicit vs. Implicit). We instantiate this taxonomy into a fine-grained evaluation framework that decomposes the trajectory to rigorously quantify these hallucinations. Leveraging this framework to isolate 100 distinctively hallucination-prone tasks including adversarial scenarios, we curate DeepHalluBench. Experiments on six state-of-theart DRAs reveal that no system achieves robust reliability. Furthermore, our diagnostic analysis traces the etiology of these failures to systemic deficits, specifically hallucination propagation and cognitive biases, providing foundational insights to guide future architectural optimization. Data and code are available at https://github.com/yuhao-zhan/DeepHalluBench.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: Diagnosing the failure mechanisms of Deep Research Agents (DRAs) remains a critical challenge. Existing benchmarks predominantly rely on end-to-end evaluation, obscuring critical intermediate hallucinations, such as flawed planning, that accumulate throughout the research trajectory. To bridge this gap, we propose a shift from outcome-based to process-aware evaluation by auditing the full research trajectory. We introduce the PIES Taxonomy to categorize hallucinations along functional components (Planning vs. Summarization) and error properties (Explicit vs. Implicit). We instantiate this taxonomy into a fine-grained evaluation framework that decomposes the trajectory to rigorously quantify these hallucinations. Leveraging this framework to isolate 100 distinctively hallucination-prone tasks including adversarial scenarios, we curate DeepHalluBench. Experiments on six state-of-theart DRAs reveal that no system achieves robust reliability. Furthermore, our diagnostic analysis traces the etiology of these failures to systemic deficits, specifically hallucination propagation and cognitive biases, providing foundational insights to guide future architectural optimization. Data and code are available at https://github.com/yuhao-zhan/DeepHalluBench.

</details>


### [264] [Guided by Trajectories: Repairing and Rewarding Tool-Use Trajectories for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23032)
*Siyu Gong,Linan Yue,Weibo Gao,Fangzhou Yao,Shimin Di,Lei Feng,Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoTraj：一种两阶段框架，通过修复和奖励工具使用轨迹来自动学习工具集成推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具集成推理方法依赖于合成轨迹和稀疏的基于结果的奖励，提供的监督有限且存在偏差，需要更有效的学习框架

Method: 1) SFT阶段：生成多个候选工具使用轨迹，评估后保留高质量轨迹，用大语言模型修复低质量轨迹；2) RL阶段：基于偏好数据集训练轨迹级奖励模型，结合结果和格式奖励来优化推理行为

Result: 在现实世界基准测试中证明了AutoTraj在工具集成推理上的有效性

Conclusion: AutoTraj通过自动修复工具使用轨迹和训练轨迹级奖励模型，显著提升了大型语言模型的工具集成推理能力

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) enables large language models (LLMs) to solve complex tasks by interacting with external tools, yet existing approaches depend on high-quality synthesized trajectories selected by scoring functions and sparse outcome-based rewards, providing limited and biased supervision for learning TIR. To address these challenges, in this paper, we propose AutoTraj, a two-stage framework that automatically learns TIR by repairing and rewarding tool-use trajectories. Specifically, in the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) stage, AutoTraj generates multiple candidate tool-use trajectories for each query and evaluates them along multiple dimensions. High-quality trajectories are directly retained, while low-quality ones are repaired using a LLM (i.e., LLM-as-Repairer). The resulting repaired and high-quality trajectories form a synthetic SFT dataset, while each repaired trajectory paired with its original low-quality counterpart constitutes a dataset for trajectory preference modeling. In the reinforcement learning (RL) stage, based on the preference dataset, we train a trajectory-level reward model to assess the quality of reasoning paths and combine it with outcome and format rewards, thereby explicitly guiding the optimization toward reliable TIR behaviors. Experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of AutoTraj in TIR.

</details>


### [265] [The Hot Mess of AI: How Does Misalignment Scale With Model Intelligence and Task Complexity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23045)
*Alexander Hägele,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Henry Sleight,Ethan Perez,Jascha Sohl-Dickstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: As AI becomes more capable, we entrust it with more general and consequential tasks. The risks from failure grow more severe with increasing task scope. It is therefore important to understand how extremely capable AI models will fail: Will they fail by systematically pursuing goals we do not intend? Or will they fail by being a hot mess, and taking nonsensical actions that do not further any goal? We operationalize this question using a bias-variance decomposition of the errors made by AI models: An AI's \emph{incoherence} on a task is measured over test-time randomness as the fraction of its error that stems from variance rather than bias in task outcome. Across all tasks and frontier models we measure, the longer models spend reasoning and taking actions, \emph{the more incoherent} their failures become. Incoherence changes with model scale in a way that is experiment dependent. However, in several settings, larger, more capable models are more incoherent than smaller models. Consequently, scale alone seems unlikely to eliminate incoherence. Instead, as more capable AIs pursue harder tasks, requiring more sequential action and thought, our results predict failures to be accompanied by more incoherent behavior. This suggests a future where AIs sometimes cause industrial accidents (due to unpredictable misbehavior), but are less likely to exhibit consistent pursuit of a misaligned goal. This increases the relative importance of alignment research targeting reward hacking or goal misspecification.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: As AI becomes more capable, we entrust it with more general and consequential tasks. The risks from failure grow more severe with increasing task scope. It is therefore important to understand how extremely capable AI models will fail: Will they fail by systematically pursuing goals we do not intend? Or will they fail by being a hot mess, and taking nonsensical actions that do not further any goal? We operationalize this question using a bias-variance decomposition of the errors made by AI models: An AI's \emph{incoherence} on a task is measured over test-time randomness as the fraction of its error that stems from variance rather than bias in task outcome. Across all tasks and frontier models we measure, the longer models spend reasoning and taking actions, \emph{the more incoherent} their failures become. Incoherence changes with model scale in a way that is experiment dependent. However, in several settings, larger, more capable models are more incoherent than smaller models. Consequently, scale alone seems unlikely to eliminate incoherence. Instead, as more capable AIs pursue harder tasks, requiring more sequential action and thought, our results predict failures to be accompanied by more incoherent behavior. This suggests a future where AIs sometimes cause industrial accidents (due to unpredictable misbehavior), but are less likely to exhibit consistent pursuit of a misaligned goal. This increases the relative importance of alignment research targeting reward hacking or goal misspecification.

</details>


### [266] [From Abstract to Contextual: What LLMs Still Cannot Do in Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23048)
*Bowen Cao,Dongdong Zhang,Yixia Li,Junpeng Liu,Shijue Huang,Chufan Shi,Hongyuan Lu,Yaokang Wu,Guanhua Chen,Wai Lam,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Large language models now solve many benchmark math problems at near-expert levels, yet this progress has not fully translated into reliable performance in real-world applications. We study this gap through contextual mathematical reasoning, where the mathematical core must be formulated from descriptive scenarios. We introduce ContextMATH, a benchmark that repurposes AIME and MATH-500 problems into two contextual settings: Scenario Grounding (SG), which embeds abstract problems into realistic narratives without increasing reasoning complexity, and Complexity Scaling (CS), which transforms explicit conditions into sub-problems to capture how constraints often appear in practice. Evaluating 61 proprietary and open-source models, we observe sharp drops: on average, open-source models decline by 13 and 34 points on SG and CS, while proprietary models drop by 13 and 20. Error analysis shows that errors are dominated by incorrect problem formulation, with formulation accuracy declining as original problem difficulty increases. Correct formulation emerges as a prerequisite for success, and its sufficiency improves with model scale, indicating that larger models advance in both understanding and reasoning. Nevertheless, formulation and reasoning remain two complementary bottlenecks that limit contextual mathematical problem solving. Finally, we find that fine-tuning with scenario data improves performance, whereas formulation-only training is ineffective. However, performance gaps are only partially alleviated, highlighting contextual mathematical reasoning as a central unsolved challenge for LLMs.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: Large language models now solve many benchmark math problems at near-expert levels, yet this progress has not fully translated into reliable performance in real-world applications. We study this gap through contextual mathematical reasoning, where the mathematical core must be formulated from descriptive scenarios. Evaluating 61 proprietary and open-source models, we observe sharp drops: on average, open-source models decline by 13 and 34 points on SG and CS, while proprietary models drop by 13 and 20. Finally, we find that fine-tuning with scenario data improves performance, whereas formulation-only training is ineffective. ### [267] [MedMCP-Calc: Benchmarking LLMs for Realistic Medical Calculator Scenarios via MCP Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23049)
*Yakun Zhu,Yutong Huang,Shengqian Qin,Zhongzhen Huang,Shaoting Zhang,Xiaofan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Medical calculators are fundamental to quantitative, evidence-based clinical practice. However, their real-world use is an adaptive, multi-stage process, requiring proactive EHR data acquisition, scenario-dependent calculator selection, and multi-step computation, whereas current benchmarks focus only on static single-step calculations with explicit instructions. To address these limitations, we introduce MedMCP-Calc, the first benchmark for evaluating LLMs in realistic medical calculator scenarios through Model Context Protocol (MCP) integration. MedMCP-Calc comprises 118 scenario tasks across 4 clinical domains, featuring fuzzy task descriptions mimicking natural queries, structured EHR database interaction, external reference retrieval, and process-level evaluation. Our evaluation of 23 leading models reveals critical limitations: even top performers like Claude Opus 4.5 exhibit substantial gaps, including difficulty selecting appropriate calculators for end-to-end workflows given fuzzy queries, poor performance in iterative SQL-based database interactions, and marked reluctance to leverage external tools for numerical computation. Performance also varies considerably across clinical domains. Building on these findings, we develop CalcMate, a fine-tuned model incorporating scenario planning and tool augmentation, achieving state-of-the-art performance among open-source models. Benchmark and Codes are available in https://github.com/SPIRAL-MED/MedMCP-Calc.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Benchmark and Codes are available in https://github.com/SPIRAL-MED/MedMCP-Calc.
